{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011Ced9VXSLR9orDKuKJH3R7","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01TWwWz8mwQArmZbNDqSeGDn","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/home/llmtest/evals/20260901-162328/cahn-hilliard_claude-opus-5-cc-medium_omp_r1/cahn-hilliard/cahn_hilliard.cpp","content":"#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <omp.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n// 3D index calculation\ninline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// True if v is an exact power of two, i.e. multiplying by 1/v gives bit-identical\n// results to dividing by v. Used to hoist the stencil divisions out of the loops\n// without changing the produced values.\nstatic bool isPowerOfTwo(const double v) noexcept {\n    if (!(v > 0.0) || std::isinf(v)) return false;\n    int e = 0;\n    return std::frexp(v, &e) == 0.5;\n}\n\n// Applies the 7-point Laplacian stencil (clamped boundaries) over the whole grid and\n// stores op(idx, center, laplacian) into dst. The (z, y) plane rows are distributed\n// over the OpenMP threads with a static schedule, which also fixes the first-touch\n// page placement used at initialization time.\ntemplate <bool Recip, typename Op>\nstatic void stencilSweep(const double* __restrict__ src, double* __restrict__ dst,\n                         const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                         const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, Op op) {\n    const double dx2 = dx * dx;\n    const double dy2 = dy * dy;\n    const double dz2 = dz * dz;\n    const double rdx2 = 1.0 / dx2;\n    const double rdy2 = 1.0 / dy2;\n    const double rdz2 = 1.0 / dz2;\n    const size_t nxy = nx * ny;\n\n    #pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            const size_t zn = (z > 0) ? z - 1 : 0;\n            const size_t zp = (z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : z;\n            const size_t yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;\n            const size_t yp = (y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;\n\n            const size_t base = z * nxy + y * nx;\n            const double* __restrict__ c0 = src + base;\n            const double* __restrict__ cyn = src + z * nxy + yn * nx;\n            const double* __restrict__ cyp = src + z * nxy + yp * nx;\n            const double* __restrict__ czn = src + zn * nxy + y * nx;\n            const double* __restrict__ czp = src + zp * nxy + y * nx;\n            double* __restrict__ out = dst + base;\n\n            // Transverse (y/z) contribution is uniform along the row, the x direction\n            // only needs its neighbour clamping at the two ends of the row.\n            const auto body = [&](const size_t x, const size_t xn, const size_t xp) {\n                const double cv = c0[x];\n                const double sxx = c0[xp] + c0[xn] - 2.0 * cv;\n                const double syy = cyp[x] + cyn[x] - 2.0 * cv;\n                const double szz = czp[x] + czn[x] - 2.0 * cv;\n                const double cxx = Recip ? sxx * rdx2 : sxx / dx2;\n                const double cyy = Recip ? syy * rdy2 : syy / dy2;\n                const double czz = Recip ? szz * rdz2 : szz / dz2;\n                out[x] = op(base + x, cv, cxx + cyy + czz);\n            };\n\n            if (nx == 1) {\n                body(0, 0, 0);\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            body(0, 0, 1);\n            #pragma omp simd\n            for (size_t x = 1; x + 1 < nx; ++x) {\n                const double cv = c0[x];\n                const double sxx = c0[x + 1] + c0[x - 1] - 2.0 * cv;\n                const double syy = cyp[x] + cyn[x] - 2.0 * cv;\n                const double szz = czp[x] + czn[x] - 2.0 * cv;\n                const double cxx = Recip ? sxx * rdx2 : sxx / dx2;\n                const double cyy = Recip ? syy * rdy2 : syy / dy2;\n                const double czz = Recip ? szz * rdz2 : szz / dz2;\n                out[x] = op(base + x, cv, cxx + cyy + czz);\n            }\n            body(nx - 1, nx - 2, nx - 1);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Dispatch to the multiply-by-reciprocal variant whenever it is bit-exact\ntemplate <typename Op>\nstatic inline void stencilSweepDispatch(const double* __restrict__ src, double* __restrict__ dst,\n                                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                                        const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, Op op) {\n    if (isPowerOfTwo(dx * dx) && isPowerOfTwo(dy * dy) && isPowerOfTwo(dz * dz)) {\n        stencilSweep<true>(src, dst, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, op);\n    } else {\n        stencilSweep<false>(src, dst, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, op);\n    }\n}\n\n// Compute chemical potential\nvoid computeChemicalPotential(const double* __restrict__ c, double* __restrict__ mu,\n                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {\n    stencilSweepDispatch(c, mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz,\n                         [=](const size_t, const double cv, const double lap) {\n                             return 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)\n                                    + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * lap;\n                         });\n}\n\n// Cahn-Hilliard update step\nvoid cahnHilliardUpdate(double* __restrict__ cnew, const double* __restrict__ cold,\n                        const double* __restrict__ mu,\n                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {\n    stencilSweepDispatch(mu, cnew, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz,\n                         [=](const size_t idx, const double, const double lap) {\n                             return cold[idx] + dt * D * lap;\n                         });\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field (also performs the NUMA first touch of the buffer)\nvoid initializeConcentration(double* __restrict__ c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    #pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n                const size_t linear_id = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Touch a scratch buffer with the same static distribution used by the kernels\nstatic void firstTouch(double* Used to hoist the stencil divisions out of the loops\n// without changing the produced values.\nstatic bool isPowerOfTwo(const double v) noexcept {\n    if (!(v > 0.0) || std::isinf(v)) return false;\n    int e = 0;\n    return std::frexp(v, &e) == 0.5;\n}\n\n// Applies the 7-point Laplacian stencil (clamped boundaries) over the whole grid and\n// stores op(idx, center, laplacian) into dst. The (z, y) plane rows are distributed\n// over the OpenMP threads with a static schedule, which also fixes the first-touch\n// page placement used at initialization time.\ntemplate <bool Recip, typename Op>\nstatic void stencilSweep(const double* __restrict__ src, double* __restrict__ dst,\n                         const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                         const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, Op op) {\n    const double dx2 = dx * dx;\n    const double dy2 = dy * dy;\n    const double dz2 = dz * dz;\n    const double rdx2 = 1.0 / dx2;\n    const double rdy2 = 1.0 / dy2;\n    const double rdz2 = 1.0 / dz2;\n    const size_t nxy = nx * ny;\n\n    #pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) : 0;\n            const size_t yp = (y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;\n\n            const size_t base = z * nxy + y * nx;\n            const double* __restrict__ c0 = src + base;\n            const double* __restrict__ cyn = src + z * nxy + yn * nx;\n            const double* __restrict__ cyp = src + z * nxy + yp * nx;\n            const double* __restrict__ czn = src + zn * nxy + y * nx;\n            const double* __restrict__ czp = src + zp * nxy + y * nx;\n            double* __restrict__ out = dst + base;\n\n            // Transverse (y/z) contribution is uniform along the row, the x direction\n            // only needs its neighbour clamping at the two ends of the row.\n            const auto body = [&](const size_t x, const size_t xn, const size_t xp) {\n                const 2.0 * cv;\n                const double cxx = Recip ? sxx * rdx2 : sxx / dx2;\n                const double cyy = Recip ? syy * rdy2 : syy / dy2;\n                const double czz = Recip ? szz * rdz2 : szz / dz2;\n                out[x] = op(base + x, cv, cxx + cyy + czz);\n            };\n\n            if (nx == 1) {\n                body(0, 0, 0);\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            body(0, 0, 1);\n            #pragma omp simd\n            for (size_t x = 1; x + 1 < nx; ++x) {\n                const double cv = c0[x];\n                const double sxx = c0[x + 1] + c0[x - 1] - 2.0 * cv;\n                const double syy = cyp[x] + cyn[x] - 2.0 * cv;\n                const double szz = czp[x] + czn[x] - 2.0 * cv;\n                const double cxx = Recip ? sxx * rdx2 : sxx / dx2;\n                const double cyy = Recip ? syy * rdy2 : syy / dy2;\n                const double czz = Recip ? szz * rdz2 : szz / dz2;\n                out[x] = op(base + x, cv, cxx + cyy + czz);\n            }\n            body(nx - 1, nx - 2, nx - 1);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Dispatch to the multiply-by-reciprocal variant whenever it is bit-exact\ntemplate <typename Op>\nstatic inline void stencilSweepDispatch(const double* __restrict__ src, double* __restrict__ dst,\n                                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                                        const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, Op op) {\n    if (isPowerOfTwo(dx * dx) && isPowerOfTwo(dy * dy) && isPowerOfTwo(dz * dz)) {\n        stencilSweep<true>(src, Used to hoist the stencil divisions out of the loops\n// without changing the produced values.\nstatic bool isPowerOfTwo(const double v) noexcept {\n    if (!(v > 0.0) || std::isinf(v)) return false;\n    int e = 0;\n    return std::frexp(v, &e) == 0.5;\n}\n\n// Applies the 7-point Laplacian stencil (clamped boundaries) over the whole grid and\n// stores op(idx, center, laplacian) into dst. The (z, y) plane rows are distributed\n// over the OpenMP threads with a static schedule, which also fixes the first-touch\n// page placement used at initialization time.\ntemplate <bool Recip, typename Op>\nstatic void stencilSweep(const double* __restrict__ src, double* __restrict__ dst,\n                         const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                         const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, Op op) {\n    const double dx2 = dx * dx;\n    const double dy2 = dy * dy;\n    const double dz2 = dz * dz;\n    const double rdx2 = 1.0 / dx2;\n    const double rdy2 = 1.0 / dy2;\n    const double rdz2 = 1.0 / dz2;\n    const size_t nxy = nx * ny;\n\n    #pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            const size_t zn = (z > 0) ? z - 1 : 0;\n            const size_t zp = (z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : z;\n            const size_t yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;\n            const size_t yp = (y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;\n\n            const size_t base = z * nxy + y * nx;\n            const double* __restrict__ c0 = src + base;\n            const double* __restrict__ cyn = src + z * nxy + yn * nx;\n            const double* __restrict__ cyp = src + z * nxy + yp * nx;\n            const double* __restrict__ czn = src + zn * nxy + y * nx;\n            const double* __restrict__ czp = src + zp * nxy + y * nx;\n            double* __restrict__ out = dst + base;\n\n            // Transverse (y/z) contribution is uniform along the row, the x direction\n            // only needs its neighbour clamping at the two ends of the row.\n            const auto body = [&](const size_t x, const size_t xn, const size_t xp) {\n                const double cv = c0[x];\n                const double sxx = c0[xp] + c0[xn] - 2.0 * cv;\n                const double syy = cyp[x] + cyn[x] - 2.0 * cv;\n                const double szz = czp[x] + czn[x] - 2.0 * cv;\n                const double cxx = Recip ? sxx * rdx2 : sxx / dx2;\n                const double cyy = Recip ? syy * rdy2 : syy / dy2;\n                const double czz = Recip ? szz * rdz2 : szz / dz2;\n                out[x] = op(base + x, cv, cxx + cyy + czz);\n            };\n\n            if (nx == 1) {\n                body(0, 0, 0);\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            body(0, 0, 1);\n            #pragma omp simd\n            for (size_t x = 1; x + 1 < nx; ++x) {\n                const double cv = c0[x];\n                const double sxx = c0[x + 1] + c0[x - 1] - 2.0 * cv;\n                const double syy = cyp[x] + cyn[x] - 2.0 * cv;\n                const double szz = czp[x] + czn[x] - 2.0 * cv;\n                const double cxx = Recip ? sxx * rdx2 : sxx / dx2;\n                const double cyy = Recip ? syy * rdy2 : syy / dy2;\n                const double czz = Recip ? szz * rdz2 : szz / dz2;\n                out[x] = op(base + x, cv, cxx + cyy + czz);\n            }\n            body(nx - 1, nx - 2, nx - 1);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Dispatch to the multiply-by-reciprocal variant whenever it is bit-exact\ntemplate <typename Op>\nstatic inline void stencilSweepDispatch(const double* __restrict__ src, double* __restrict__ dst,\n                                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                                        const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, Op op) {\n    if (isPowerOfTwo(dx * dx) && isPowerOfTwo(dy * dy) && isPowerOfTwo(dz * dz)) {\n        stencilSweep<true>(src, dst, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, op);\n    } else {\n        stencilSweep<false>(src, dst, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, op);\n    }\n}\n\n// Compute chemical potential\nvoid computeChemicalPotential(const double* __restrict__ c, double* __restrict__ mu,\n                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {\n    stencilSweepDispatch(c, mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz,\n                         [=](const size_t, const double cv, const double lap) {\n                             return 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)\n                                    + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * lap;\n                         });\n}\n\n// Cahn-Hilliard update step\nvoid cahnHilliardUpdate(double* __restrict__ cnew, const double* __restrict__ cold,\n                        const double* __restrict__ mu,\n                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {\n    stencilSweepDispatch(mu, cnew, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz,\n                         [=](const size_t idx, const double, const double lap) {\n                             return cold[idx] + dt * D * lap;\n                         });\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field (also performs the NUMA first touch of the buffer)\nvoid initializeConcentration(double* __restrict__ c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    #pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n                const size_t linear_id = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Touch a scratch buffer with the same static distribution used by the kernels\nstatic void firstTouch(double* __restrict__ p, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    #pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            double* __restrict__ row = p + z * nx * ny + y * nx;\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                row[x] = 0.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const double* __restrict__ c, const size_t gridSize) {\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    int bad = 0;\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(|:bad)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < gridSize; ++i) {\n        bad |= (std::isnan(c[i]) || std::isinf(c[i])) ? 1 : 0;\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return If the user did not configure the OpenMP\n// affinity, pin the threads ourselves: one thread per physical core, spread over\n// all packages, using the SMT siblings only once every core is occupied.\nstatic void bindThreadsIfUnconfigured() {\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_PROC_BIND\") != nullptr || getenv(\"OMP_PLACES\") != nullptr ||\n        getenv(\"GOMP_CPU_AFFINITY\") != nullptr || getenv(\"KMP_AFFINITY\") != nullptr) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    cpu_set_t allowed;\n    CPU_ZERO(&allowed);\n    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(allowed), &allowed) != 0) return;\n\n    // Group the usable CPUs into \"rounds\": round r holds the r-th SMT sibling of\n    // every physical core, so round 0 already covers all cores exactly once.\n    std::vector<std::vector<int>> rounds;\n    std::vector<std::pair<long, long>> seenCores; // (package, core) -> round index by count\n    std::vector<int> coreCount;\n    for (int cpu = 0; cpu < CPU_SETSIZE; ++cpu) {\n        if (!CPU_ISSET(cpu, &allowed)) continue;\n\n        long pkg = 0;\n        long core = cpu;\n        char path[128];\n        snprintf(path, sizeof(path), \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/physical_package_id\", cpu);\n        if (FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\")) {\n            if (fscanf(f, \"%ld\", &pkg) != 1) pkg = 0;\n            fclose(f);\n        }\n        snprintf(path, sizeof(path), \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/core_id\", cpu);\n        if (FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\")) {\n            if (fscanf(f, \"%ld\", &core) != 1) core = cpu;\n            fclose(f);\n        }\n\n        size_t g = 0;\n        for (; g < seenCores.size(); ++g) {\n            if (seenCores[g].first == pkg && seenCores[g].second == core) break;\n        }\n        if (g == seenCores.size()) {\n            seenCores.emplace_back(pkg, core);\n            coreCount.push_back(0);\n        }\n        const size_t r = static_cast<size_t>(coreCount[g]++);\n        if (r >= rounds.size()) rounds.resize(r + 1);\n        rounds[r].push_back(cpu);\n    }\n    if (rounds.empty() || rounds[0].empty()) return;\n\n    const size_t nrounds = rounds.size();\n    const size_t ncores = rounds[0].size();\n\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const size_t n = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_num_threads());\n        const size_t tid = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_thread_num());\n\n        size_t r = 0;\n        size_t i = tid;\n        size_t perRound = n;\n        if (n > ncores) {\n            perRound = (n + nrounds - 1) / nrounds;\n            r = std::min(tid / perRound, nrounds - 1);\n            i = tid % perRound;\n        }\n        const std::vector<int>& ring = rounds[r];\n        const int cpu = ring[std::min(i * ring.size() / std::max<size_t>(perRound, 1), ring.size() - 1)];\n\n        cpu_set_t one;\n        CPU_ZERO(&one);\n        CPU_SET(cpu, &one);\n        pthread_setaffinity_np(pthread_self(), sizeof(one), &one);\n    }\n}"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":413,"cache_read_input_tokens":35576,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":413},"output_tokens":7,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"cb1eade5-90f2-4131-8d17-30209b0370ad","uuid":"7602509c-6283-4977-bd6b-73771c31fb1b","timestamp":"2026-09-01T17:57:20.736Z","request_id":"req_011Ced9hKTbCbmW5bXEnorqm"}

g < seenCores.size(); ++g) {\n            if (seenCores[g].first == pkg && seenCores[g].second == core) break;\n        }\n        if (g == seenCores.size()) {\n            seenCores.emplace_back(pkg, core);\n            coreCount.push_back(0);\n        }\n        const size_t r = static_cast<size_t>(coreCount[g]++);\n        if (r >= rounds.size()) rounds.resize(r + 1);\n        rounds[r].push_back(cpu);\n    }\n    if (rounds.empty() || rounds[0].empty()) return;\n\n    const size_t nrounds = rounds.size();\n    const size_t ncores = rounds[0].size();\n\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const size_t n = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_num_threads());\n        const size_t tid = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_thread_num());\n\n        size_t r = 0;\n        size_t i = tid;\n        size_t perRound = n;\n        if (n > ncores) {\n            perRound = (n + nrounds - 1) / nrounds;\n            r = std::min(tid / perRound, nrounds - 1);\n            i = tid % perRound;\n        }\n        const std::vector<int>& ring = rounds[r];\n        const int cpu = ring[std::min(i * ring.size() / std::max<size_t>(perRound, 1), ring.size() - 1)];\n\n        cpu_set_t one;\n        CPU_ZERO(&one);\n        CPU_SET(cpu, &one);\n        pthread_setaffinity_np(pthread_self(), sizeof(one), &one);\n    }\n}","originalFile":"#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <omp.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n// 3D index calculation\ninline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// True if v is an exact power of two, i.e. multiplying by 1/v gives bit-identical\n// results to dividing by v. Used to hoist the stencil divisions out of the loops\n// without changing the produced values.\nstatic bool isPowerOfTwo(const double v) noexcept {\n    if (!(v > 0.0) || std::isinf(v)) return false;\n    int e = 0;\n    return std::frexp(v, &e) == 0.5;\n}\n\n// Applies the 7-point Laplacian stencil (clamped boundaries) over the whole grid and\n// stores op(idx, center, laplacian) into dst. The (z, y) plane rows are distributed\n// over the OpenMP threads with a static schedule, which also fixes the first-touch\n// page placement used at initialization time.\ntemplate <bool Recip, typename Op>\nstatic void stencilSweep(const double* __restrict__ src, double* __restrict__ dst,\n                         const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                         const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, Op op) {\n    const double dx2 = dx * dx;\n    const double dy2 = dy * dy;\n    const double dz2 = dz * dz;\n    const double rdx2 = 1.0 / dx2;\n    const double rdy2 = 1.0 / dy2;\n    const double rdz2 = 1.0 / dz2;\n    const size_t nxy = nx * ny;\n\n    #pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            const size_t zn = (z > 0) ? z - 1 : 0;\n            const size_t zp = (z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : z;\n            const size_t yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;\n            const size_t yp = (y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;\n\n            const size_t base = z * nxy + y * nx;\n            const double* __restrict__ c0 = src + base;\n            const double* __restrict__ cyn = src + z * nxy + yn * nx;\n            const double* __restrict__ cyp = src + z * nxy + yp * nx;\n            const double* __restrict__ czn = src + zn * nxy + y * nx;\n            const double* __restrict__ czp = src + zp * nxy + y * nx;\n            double* __restrict__ out = dst + base;\n\n            // Transverse (y/z) contribution is uniform along the row, the x direction\n            // only needs its neighbour clamping at the two ends of the row.\n            const auto body = [&](const size_t x, const size_t xn, const size_t xp) {\n                const double cv = c0[x];\n                const double sxx = c0[xp] + c0[xn] - 2.0 * cv;\n                const double syy = cyp[x] + cyn[x] - 2.0 * cv;\n                const double szz = czp[x] + czn[x] - 2.0 * cv;\n                const double cxx = Recip ? sxx * rdx2 : sxx / dx2;\n                const double cyy = Recip ? syy * rdy2 : syy / dy2;\n                const double czz = Recip ? szz * rdz2 : szz / dz2;\n                out[x] = op(base + x, cv, cxx + cyy + czz);\n            };\n\n            if (nx == 1) {\n                body(0, 0, 0);\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            body(0, 0, 1);\n            const size_t xend = nx - 1;\n            #pragma omp simd\n            for (size_t x = 1; x < xend; ++x) {\n                const double cv = c0[x];\n const double cxx = Recip ? sxx * rdx2 : sxx / dx2;\n                const double cyy = Recip ? syy * rdy2 : syy / dy2;\n                const double czz = Recip ? szz * rdz2 : szz / dz2;\n                out[x] = op(base + x, cv, cxx + cyy + czz);\n            }\n            body(nx - 1, nx - 2, nx - 1);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Dispatch to the multiply-by-reciprocal variant whenever it is bit-exact\ntemplate <typename Op>\nstatic inline void stencilSweepDispatch(const double* __restrict__ src, double* __restrict__ dst,\n                                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                                        const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, Op op) {\n    if (isPowerOfTwo(dx * dx) && isPowerOfTwo(dy * dy) && isPowerOfTwo(dz * dz)) {\n        stencilSweep<true>(src, dst, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, op);\n    } else {\n        stencilSweep<false>(src, dst, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, op);\n    }\n}\n\n// Compute                  const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {\n    stencilSweepDispatch(c, mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz,\n                         [=](const size_t, const double cv, const double lap) {\n                             return 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)\n                                    + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * lap;\n                         });\n}\n\n// Cahn-Hilliard update step\nvoid cahnHilliardUpdate(double* __restrict__ cnew, const double* __restrict__ cold,\n                        const double* __restrict__ mu,\n                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {\n    stencilSweepDispatch(mu, cnew, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz,\n                         [=](const size_t idx, const double, const double lap) {\n                             return cold[idx] + dt * D * lap;\n                         });\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field (also performs the NUMA first touch of the buffer)\nvoid initializeConcentration(double* __restrict__ c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    #pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Touch a scratch buffer with If the user did not configure the OpenMP\n// affinity, pin the threads ourselves: one thread per physical core, spread over\n// all packages, using the SMT siblings only once every core is occupied.\nstatic void bindThreadsIfUnconfigured() {\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_PROC_BIND\") != nullptr || getenv(\"OMP_PLACES\") != nullptr ||\n        getenv(\"GOMP_CPU_AFFINITY\") != nullptr || getenv(\"KMP_AFFINITY\") != nullptr) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    cpu_set_t allowed;\n    CPU_ZERO(&allowed);\n    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(allowed), &allowed) != 0) return;\n\n    // Group the usable CPUs into \"rounds\": round r holds the r-th SMT sibling of\n    // every physical core, so round 0 already covers all cores exactly once.\n    std::vector<std::vector<int>> rounds;\n    std::vector<std::pair<long, long>> seenCores; // (package, core) -> round index by count\n    std::vector<int> coreCount;\n    for (int cpu = 0; cpu < CPU_SETSIZE; ++cpu) {\n        if (!CPU_ISSET(cpu, &allowed)) continue;\n\n        long pkg = 0;\n        long core = cpu;\n        char path[128];\n        snprintf(path, sizeof(path), \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/physical_package_id\", cpu);\n        if (FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\")) {\n            if (fscanf(f, \"%ld\", &pkg) != 1) pkg = 0;\n            fclose(f);\n        }\n        snprintf(path, sizeof(path), \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/core_id\", cpu);\n        if (FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\")) {\n            if (fscanf(f, \"%ld\", &core) != 1) core = cpu;\n            fclose(f);\n        }\n\n        size_t g = 0;\n        for (; g < seenCores.size(); ++g) {\n            if (seenCores[g].first == pkg && seenCores[g].second == core) break;\n        }\n        if (g == seenCores.size()) {\n            seenCores.emplace_back(pkg, core);\n            coreCount.push_back(0);\n        }\n        const size_t r = static_cast<size_t>(coreCount[g]++);\n        if (r >= rounds.size()) rounds.resize(r + 1);\n        rounds[r].push_back(cpu);\n    }\n    if (rounds.empty() || rounds[0].empty()) return;\n\n    const size_t nrounds = rounds.size();\n    const size_t ncores = rounds[0].size();\n\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const size_t n = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_num_threads());\n        const size_t tid = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_thread_num());\n\n        size_t r = 0;\n        size_t i = tid;\n        size_t perRound = n;\n        if (n > ncores) {\n            perRound = (n + nrounds - 1) / nrounds;\n            r = std::min(tid / perRound, nrounds - 1);\n            i = tid % perRound;\n        }\n        const std::vector<int>& ring = rounds[r];\n        const int cpu = ring[std::min(i * ring.size() / std::max<size_t>(perRound, 1), ring.size() - 1)];\n\n        cpu_set_t one;\n        CPU_ZERO(&one);\n        CPU_SET(cpu, &one);\n        pthread_setaffinity_np(pthread_self(), sizeof(one), &one);\n    }\n}\n\n// 3D index calculation\ninline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// True if v is an exact power of two, i.e. multiplying by 1/v gives bit-identical\n// results to dividing by v. Used to hoist the stencil divisions out of the loops\n// without changing the produced values.\nstatic bool isPowerOfTwo(const double v) noexcept {\n    if (!(v > 0.0) || std::isinf(v)) return false;\n    int e = 0;\n    return std::frexp(v, &e) == 0.5;\n}\n\n// Applies the 7-point Laplacian stencil (clamped boundaries) over the whole grid and\n// stores op(idx, center, laplacian) into dst. The (z, y) plane rows are distributed\n// over the OpenMP threads with a static schedule, which also fixes the first-touch\n// page placement used at initialization time.\ntemplate <bool Recip, typename Op>\nstatic void stencilSweep(const double* __restrict__ src, double* __restrict__ dst,\n                         const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                         const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, Op op) {\n    const double dx2 = dx * dx;\n    const double dy2 = dy * dy;\n    const double dz2 = dz * dz;\n    const double rdx2 = 1.0 / dx2;\n    const double rdy2 = 1.0 / dy2;\n    const double rdz2 = 1.0 / dz2;\n    const size_t nxy = nx * ny;\n\n    #pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            const size_t zn = (z > 0) ? z - 1 : 0;\n            const size_t zp = (z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : z;\n            const size_t yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;\n            const size_t yp = (y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;\n\n            const size_t base = z * nxy + y * nx;\n            const double* __restrict__ c0 = src + base;\n            const double* __restrict__ cyn = src + z * nxy + yn * nx;\n            const double* __restrict__ cyp = src + z * nxy + yp * nx;\n            const double* __restrict__ czn = src + zn * nxy + y * nx;\n            const double* __restrict__ czp = src + zp * nxy + y * nx;\n            double* __restrict__ out = dst + base;\n\n            // Transverse (y/z) contribution is uniform along the row, the x direction\n            // only needs its neighbour clamping at the two ends of the row.\n            const auto body = [&](const size_t x, const size_t xn, const size_t xp) {\n                const double cv = c0[x];\n                const double sxx = c0[xp] + c0[xn] - 2.0 * cv;\n                const double syy = cyp[x] + cyn[x] - 2.0 * cv;\n                const double szz = czp[x] + czn[x] - 2.0 * cv;\n                const double cxx = Recip ? sxx * rdx2 : sxx / dx2;\n                const double cyy = Recip ? syy * rdy2 : syy / dy2;\n                const double czz = Recip ? szz * rdz2 : szz / dz2;\n                out[x] = op(base + x, cv, cxx + cyy + czz);\n            };\n\n            if (nx == 1) {\n                body(0, 0, 0);\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            body(0, 0, 1);\n            const size_t xend = nx - 1;\n            #pragma omp simd\n            for (size_t x = 1; x < xend; ++x) {\n                const double cv = c0[x];\n                const double sxx = c0[x + 1] + c0[x - 1] - 2.0 * cv;\n                const double syy = cyp[x] + cyn[x] - 2.0 * cv;\n                const double szz = czp[x] + czn[x] - 2.0 * cv;\n                const double cxx = Recip ? sxx * rdx2 : sxx / dx2;\n                const double cyy = Recip ? syy * rdy2 : syy / dy2;\n                const double czz = Recip ? szz * rdz2 : szz / dz2;\n                out[x] = op(base + x, cv, cxx + cyy + czz);\n            }\n            body(nx - 1, nx - 2, nx - 1);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Dispatch to the multiply-by-reciprocal variant whenever it is bit-exact\ntemplate <typename Op>\nstatic inline void stencilSweepDispatch(const double* __restrict__ src, double* __restrict__ dst,\n                                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                                        const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, Op op) {\n    if (isPowerOfTwo(dx * dx) && isPowerOfTwo(dy * dy) && isPowerOfTwo(dz * dz)) {\n        stencilSweep<true>(src, dst, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, op);\n    } else {\n        stencilSweep<false>(src, dst, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, op);\n    }\n}\n\n// Compute chemical potential\nvoid computeChemicalPotential(const double* __restrict__ c, double* __restrict__ mu,\n                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {\n    stencilSweepDispatch(c, mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz,\n                         [=](const size_t, const double cv, const double lap) {\n                             return 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)\n                                    + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * lap;\n                         });\n}\n\n// Cahn-Hilliard update step\nvoid cahnHilliardUpdate(double* __restrict__ cnew, const double* __restrict__ cold,\n                        const double* __restrict__ mu,\n                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {\n    stencilSweepDispatch(mu, cnew, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz,\n                         [=](const size_t idx, const double, const double lap) {\n                             return cold[idx] + dt * D * lap;\n                         });\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field (also performs the NUMA first touch of the buffer)\nvoid initializeConcentration(double* __restrict__ c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    #pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n                const size_t linear_id = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Touch a scratch buffer with the same static distribution used by the kernels\nstatic void firstTouch(double* __restrict__ p, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    #pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            double* __restrict__ row = p + z * nx * ny + y * nx;\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                row[x] = 0.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const double* __restrict__ c, const size_t gridSize) {\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    int bad = 0;\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(|:bad)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < gridSize; ++i) {\n        bad |= (std::isnan(c[i]) || std::isinf(c[i])) ? 1 : 0;\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min:minVal) reduction(max:maxVal)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < gridSize; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, c[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, c[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num> validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 64;\n    size_t ny = 0;\n    size_t nz = 0;\n    int iterations = 20;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-x\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-y\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-z\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;\n    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;\n\n    printf(\"Cahn-Hilliard Phase Separation Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\\n\", nx, ny, nz);\n    printf(\"Time steps: %d\\n\", iterations);\n    printf(\"Threads: %d\\n\", omp_get_max_threads());\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n\n    // Physical parameters\n    const double dx = 1.0;\n    const double dy = 1.0;\n    const double dz = 1.0;\n    const double dt = 0.01;\n    const double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double e_AB = (2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double gamma = 0.5;\n    const double D = 1.0;\n\n    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    // Allocate arrays. Raw allocations are used so that the pages are first touched\n    // by the thread that later works on them (NUMA locality).\n    const size_t bytes = ((gridSize * sizeof(double)) + 63) & ~size_t(63);\n    double* cold = static_cast<double*>(std::aligned_alloc(64, bytes));\n    double* cnew = static_cast<double*>(std::aligned_alloc(64, bytes));\n    double* mu = static_cast<double*>(std::aligned_alloc(64, bytes));\n    if (cold == nullptr || cnew == nullptr || mu == nullptr) {\n        printf(\"Allocation failed\\n\");\n        return 1;\n    }\n    firstTouch(cnew, nx, ny, nz);\n    firstTouch(mu, nx, ny, nz);\n\n    // Initialize concentration field\n    printf(\"Initializing concentration field...\\n\");\n    initializeConcentration(cold, nx, ny, nz);\n\n    // Run simulation\n    printf(\"Running Cahn-Hilliard simulation...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    for (int t = 0; If the user did not configure the OpenMP\n// affinity, pin the threads ourselves: one thread per physical core, spread over\n// all packages, using the SMT siblings only once every core is occupied.\nstatic void bindThreadsIfUnconfigured() {\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_PROC_BIND\") != nullptr || getenv(\"OMP_PLACES\") != nullptr ||\n        getenv(\"GOMP_CPU_AFFINITY\") != nullptr || getenv(\"KMP_AFFINITY\") != nullptr) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    cpu_set_t allowed;\n    CPU_ZERO(&allowed);\n    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(allowed), &allowed) != 0) return;\n\n    // Group the usable CPUs into \"rounds\": round r holds the r-th SMT sibling of\n    // every physical core, so round 0 already covers all cores exactly once.\n    std::vector<std::vector<int>> rounds;\n    std::vector<std::pair<long, long>> seenCores; // (package, core) -> round index by count\n    std::vector<int> coreCount;\n    for (int cpu = 0; cpu < CPU_SETSIZE; ++cpu) {\n        if (!CPU_ISSET(cpu, &allowed)) continue;\n\n        long pkg = 0;\n        long core = cpu;\n        char path[128];\n        snprintf(path, sizeof(path), \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/physical_package_id\", cpu);\n        if (FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\")) {\n            if (fscanf(f, \"%ld\", &pkg) != 1) pkg = 0;\n            fclose(f);\n        }\n        snprintf(path, sizeof(path), \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/core_id\", cpu);\n        if (FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\")) {\n            if (fscanf(f, \"%ld\", &core) != 1) core = cpu;\n            fclose(f);\n        }\n\n        size_t g = 0;\n        for (; g < seenCores.size(); ++g) {\n            if (seenCores[g].first == pkg && seenCores[g].second == core) break;\n        }\n        if (g == seenCores.size()) {\n            seenCores.emplace_back(pkg, core);\n            coreCount.push_back(0);\n        }\n        const size_t r = static_cast<size_t>(coreCount[g]++);\n        if (r >= rounds.size()) rounds.resize(r + 1);\n        rounds[r].push_back(cpu);\n    }\n    if (rounds.empty() || rounds[0].empty()) return;\n\n    const size_t nrounds = rounds.size();\n    const size_t ncores = rounds[0].size();\n\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const size_t n = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_num_threads());\n        const size_t tid = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_thread_num());\n\n        size_t r = 0;\n        size_t i = tid;\n        size_t perRound = n;\n        if (n > ncores) {\n            perRound = (n + nrounds - 1) / nrounds;\n            r = std::min(tid / perRound, nrounds - 1);\n            i = tid % perRound;\n        }\n        const std::vector<int>& ring = rounds[r];\n        const int cpu = ring[std::min(i * ring.size() / std::max<size_t>(perRound, 1), ring.size() - 1)];\n\n        cpu_set_t one;\n        CPU_ZERO(&one);\n        CPU_SET(cpu, &one);\n        pthread_setaffinity_np(pthread_self(), sizeof(one), &one);\n    }\n}\n\n// 3D index calculation\ninline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// True if v is an exact power of two, i.e. multiplying by 1/v gives bit-identical\n// results to dividing by v. Used to hoist the stencil divisions out of the loops\n// without changing the produced values.\nstatic bool isPowerOfTwo(const double v) noexcept {\n    if (!(v > 0.0) || std::isinf(v)) return false;\n    int e = 0;\n    return std::frexp(v, &e) == 0.5;\n}\n\n// Applies the 7-point Laplacian stencil (clamped boundaries) over the whole grid and\n// stores op(idx, center, laplacian) into dst. The (z, y) plane rows are distributed\n// over the OpenMP threads with a static schedule, which also fixes the first-touch\n// page placement used at initialization time.\ntemplate <bool Recip, typename Op>\nstatic void stencilSweep(const double* __restrict__ src, double* __restrict__ dst,\n                         const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                         const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, Op op) {\n    const double dx2 = dx * dx;\n    const double dy2 = dy * dy;\n    const double dz2 = dz * dz;\n    const double rdx2 = 1.0 / dx2;\n    const double rdy2 = 1.0 / dy2;\n    const double rdz2 = 1.0 / dz2;\n    const size_t nxy = nx * ny;\n\n    #pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            const size_t zn = (z > 0) ? z - 1 : 0;\n            const size_t zp = (z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : z;\n            const size_t yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;\n            const size_t yp = (y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;\n\n            const size_t base = z * nxy + y * nx;\n            const double* __restrict__ c0 = src + base;\n            const double* __restrict__ cyn = src + z * nxy + yn * nx;\n            const double* __restrict__ cyp = src + z * nxy + yp * nx;\n            const double* __restrict__ czn = src + zn * nxy + y * nx;\n            const double* __restrict__ czp = src + zp * nxy + y * nx;\n            double* __restrict__ out = dst + base;\n\n            // Transverse (y/z) contribution is uniform along the row, the x direction\n            // only needs its neighbour clamping at the two ends of the row.\n            const auto body = [&](const size_t x, const size_t xn, const size_t xp) {\n                const double cv = c0[x];\n                const double sxx = c0[xp] + c0[xn] - 2.0 * cv;\n                const double syy = cyp[x] + cyn[x] - 2.0 * cv;\n                const double szz = czp[x] + czn[x] - 2.0 * cv;\n                const double cxx = Recip ? sxx * rdx2 : sxx / dx2;\n                const double cyy = Recip ? syy * rdy2 : syy / dy2;\n                const double czz = Recip ? szz * rdz2 : szz / dz2;\n                out[x] = op(base + x, cv, cxx + cyy + czz);\n            };\n\n            if (nx == 1) {\n                body(0, 0, 0);\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            body(0, 0, 1);\n            const size_t xend = nx - 1;\n            #pragma omp simd\n            for (size_t x = 1; x < xend; ++x) {\n                const double cv = c0[x];\n                const double sxx = c0[x + 1] + c0[x - 1] - 2.0 * cv;\n                const double syy = cyp[x] + cyn[x] - 2.0 * cv;\n                const double szz = czp[x] + czn[x] - 2.0 * cv;\n                const double cxx = Recip ? sxx * rdx2 : sxx / dx2;\n                const double cyy = Recip ? syy * rdy2 : syy / dy2;\n                const double czz = Recip ? szz * rdz2 : szz / dz2;\n                out[x] = op(base + x, cv, cxx + cyy + czz);\n            }\n            body(nx - 1, nx - 2, nx - 1);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Dispatch to the multiply-by-reciprocal variant whenever it is bit-exact\ntemplate <typename Op>\nstatic inline void stencilSweepDispatch(const double* __restrict__ src, double* __restrict__ dst,\n                                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                                        const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, Op op) {\n    if (isPowerOfTwo(dx * dx) && isPowerOfTwo(dy * dy) && isPowerOfTwo(dz * dz)) {\n        stencilSweep<true>(src, dst, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, op);\n    } else {\n        stencilSweep<false>(src, dst, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, op);\n    }\n}\n\n// Compute                  const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {\n    stencilSweepDispatch(c, mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz,\n                         [=](const size_t, const double cv, const double lap) {\n                             return 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv cahnHilliardUpdate(double* __restrict__ cnew, const double* __restrict__ cold,\n                        const double* __restrict__ mu,\n                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const        [=](const size_t idx, const double, const double lap) {\n                             return cold[idx] + dt * D * lap;\n                         });\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field (also performs the NUMA first touch of the buffer)\nvoid initializeConcentration(double* __restrict__ c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    #pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n nx; ++x) {\n                row[x] = 0.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const double* __restrict__ c, const size_t gridSize) {\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    int bad = evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min:minVal) reduction(max:maxVal)\n If the user did not configure the OpenMP\n// affinity, pin the threads ourselves: one thread per physical core, spread over\n// all packages, using the SMT siblings only once every core is occupied.\n// The stencil saturates the memory bandwidth with one thread per core already, so\n// the SMT siblings are left idle unless the user asks for more threads.\nstatic void bindThreadsIfUnconfigured() {\n    cpu_set_t allowed;\n    CPU_ZERO(&allowed);\n    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(allowed), &allowed) != 0) return;\n\n    // Group the usable CPUs into \"rounds\": round r holds the r-th SMT sibling of\n    // every physical core, so round 0 already covers all cores exactly once.\n    std::vector<std::vector<int>> rounds;\n    std::vector<std::pair<long, long>> seenCores; // (package, core) -> round index by count\n    std::vector<int> coreCount;\n    for (int cpu = 0; cpu < CPU_SETSIZE; ++cpu) {\n        if (!CPU_ISSET(cpu, &allowed)) continue;\n\n        long pkg = 0;\n        long core = cpu;\n        char path[128];\n        snprintf(path, sizeof(path), \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/physical_package_id\", cpu);\n        if (FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\")) {\n            if (fscanf(f, \"%ld\", &pkg) != 1) pkg = 0;\n            fclose(f);\n        }\n        snprintf(path, sizeof(path), \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/core_id\", cpu);\n        if (FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\")) {\n            if (fscanf(f, \"%ld\", &core) != 1) core = cpu;\n            fclose(f);\n        }\n\n        size_t g = 0;\n        for (; g < seenCores.size(); ++g) {\n            if (seenCores[g].first == pkg && seenCores[g].second == core) break;\n        }\n        if (g == seenCores.size()) {\n            seenCores.emplace_back(pkg, core);\n            coreCount.push_back(0);\n        }\n        const size_t r = static_cast<size_t>(coreCount[g]++);\n        if (r >= rounds.size()) rounds.resize(r + 1);\n        rounds[r].push_back(cpu);\n    }\n    if (rounds.empty() || rounds[0].empty()) return;\n\n    const size_t nrounds = rounds.size();\n    const size_t ncores = rounds[0].size();\n\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const size_t n = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_num_threads());\n        const size_t tid = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_thread_num());\n\n        size_t r = 0;\n        size_t i = tid;\n        size_t perRound = n;\n        if (n > ncores) {\n            perRound = (n + nrounds - 1) / nrounds;\n            r = std::min(tid / perRound, nrounds - 1);\n            i = tid % perRound;\n        }\n        const std::vector<int>& ring = rounds[r];\n        const int cpu = ring[std::min(i * ring.size() / std::max<size_t>(perRound, 1), ring.size() - 1)];\n\n        cpu_set_t one;\n        CPU_ZERO(&one);\n        CPU_SET(cpu, &one);\n        pthread_setaffinity_np(pthread_self(), sizeof(one), &one);\n    }\n}\n\n// 3D index calculation\ninline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// True if v is an exact power of two, i.e. multiplying by 1/v gives bit-identical\n// results to dividing by v. Used to hoist the stencil divisions out of the loops\n// without changing the produced values.\nstatic bool isPowerOfTwo(const double v) noexcept {\n    if (!(v > 0.0) || std::isinf(v)) return false;\n    int e = 0;\n    return std::frexp(v, &e) == 0.5;\n}\n\n// Applies the 7-point Laplacian stencil (clamped boundaries) over the whole grid and\n// stores op(idx, center, laplacian) into dst. The (z, y) plane rows are distributed\n// over the OpenMP threads with a static schedule, which also fixes the first-touch\n// page placement used at initialization time.\ntemplate <bool Recip, typename Op>\nstatic void stencilSweep(const double* __restrict__ src, double* __restrict__ dst,\n                         const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                         const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, Op op) {\n    const double dx2 = dx * dx;\n    const double dy2 = dy * dy;\n    const double dz2 = dz * dz;\n    const double rdx2 = 1.0 / dx2;\n    const double rdy2 = 1.0 / dy2;\n    const double rdz2 = 1.0 / dz2;\n    const size_t nxy = nx * ny;\n\n    #pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            const size_t zn = (z > 0) ? z - 1 : 0;\n            const size_t zp = (z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : z;\n            const size_t yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;\n            const size_t yp = (y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;\n\n            const size_t base = z * nxy + y * nx;\n            const double* __restrict__ c0 = src + base;\n            const double* __restrict__ cyn = src + z * nxy + yn * nx;\n            const double* __restrict__ cyp = src + z * nxy + yp * nx;\n            const double* __restrict__ czn = src + zn * nxy + y * nx;\n            const double* __restrict__ czp = src + zp * nxy + y * nx;\n            double* __restrict__ out = dst + base;\n\n            // Transverse (y/z) contribution is uniform along the row, the x direction\n            // only needs its neighbour clamping at the two ends of the row.\n            const auto body = [&](const size_t x, const size_t xn, const size_t xp) {\n                const double cv = c0[x];\n                const double sxx = c0[xp] + c0[xn] - 2.0 * cv;\n                const double syy = cyp[x] + cyn[x] - 2.0 * cv;\n                const double szz = czp[x] + czn[x] - 2.0 * cv;\n                const double cxx = Recip ? sxx * rdx2 : sxx / dx2;\n                const double cyy = Recip ? syy * rdy2 : syy / dy2;\n                const double czz = Recip ? szz * rdz2 : szz / dz2;\n                out[x] = op(base + x, cv, cxx + cyy + czz);\n            };\n\n            if (nx == 1) {\n                body(0, 0, 0);\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            body(0, 0, 1);\n            const size_t xend = nx - 1;\n            #pragma omp simd\n            for (size_t x = 1; x < xend; ++x) {\n                const double cv = c0[x];\n const double cxx = Recip ? sxx * rdx2 : sxx / dx2;\n                const double cyy = Recip ? syy * rdy2 : syy / dy2;\n                const double czz = Recip ? szz * rdz2 : szz / dz2;\n                out[x] = op(base + x, cv, cxx + cyy + czz);\n            }\n            body(nx - 1, nx - 2, nx - 1);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Dispatch to the multiply-by-reciprocal variant whenever it is bit-exact\ntemplate <typename Op>\nstatic inline void stencilSweepDispatch(const double* __restrict__ src, double* __restrict__ dst,\n                                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                                        const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, Op op) {\n    if (isPowerOfTwo(dx * dx) && isPowerOfTwo(dy * dy) &&                  const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {\n    stencilSweepDispatch(c, mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz,\n                         [=](const size_t, const double cv, const double lap) {\n                             return 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)\n                                    + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * lap;\n                         });\n}\n\n// Cahn-Hilliard update step\nvoid cahnHilliardUpdate(double* __restrict__ cnew, const double* __restrict__ cold,\n                        const double* __restrict__ mu,\n                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {\n    stencilSweepDispatch(mu, cnew, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz,\n                         [=](const size_t idx, const double, const double lap) {\n                             return cold[idx] + dt * D * lap;\n                         });\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field (also performs the NUMA first touch of the buffer)\nvoid initializeConcentration(double* __restrict__ c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    #pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n The stencil\n// already saturates the memory bandwidth with one thread per core, so the default\n// team size is capped at the number of cores unless the user asks for more.","originalFile":"#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <omp.h>\n#include <pthread.h>\n#include <sched.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n// This benchmark is purely memory bound, so thread placement dominates its\n// performance on multi socket machines. If the user did not configure the OpenMP\n// affinity, pin the threads ourselves: one thread per physical core, spread over\n// all packages, using the SMT siblings only once every core is occupied.\n// The stencil saturates the memory bandwidth with one thread per core already, so\n// the SMT siblings are left idle unless the user asks for more threads.\nstatic void bindThreadsIfUnconfigured() {\n    cpu_set_t allowed;\n    CPU_ZERO(&allowed);\n    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(allowed), &allowed) != 0) return;\n\n    // Group the usable CPUs into \"rounds\": round r holds the r-th SMT sibling of\n    // every physical core, so round 0 already covers all cores exactly once.\n    std::vector<std::vector<int>> rounds;\n    std::vector<std::pair<long, long>> seenCores; // (package, core) -> round index by count\n    std::vector<int> coreCount;\n    for (int cpu = 0; cpu < CPU_SETSIZE; ++cpu) {\n        if (!CPU_ISSET(cpu, &allowed)) continue;\n\n        long pkg = 0;\n        long core = cpu;\n        char path[128];\n        snprintf(path, sizeof(path), \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/physical_package_id\", cpu);\n        if (FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\")) {\n            if (fscanf(f, \"%ld\", &pkg) != 1) pkg = 0;\n            fclose(f);\n        }\n        snprintf(path, sizeof(path), \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/core_id\", cpu);\n        if (FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\")) {\n            if (fscanf(f, \"%ld\", &core) != 1) core = cpu;\n            fclose(f);\n        }\n\n        size_t g = 0;\n        for (; g < seenCores.size(); ++g) {\n            if (seenCores[g].first == pkg && seenCores[g].second == core) break;\n        }\n        if (g == seenCores.size()) {\n            seenCores.emplace_back(pkg, core);\n            coreCount.push_back(0);\n        }\n        const size_t r = static_cast<size_t>(coreCount[g]++);\n        if (r >= rounds.size()) rounds.resize(r + 1);\n        rounds[r].push_back(cpu);\n    }\n    if (rounds.empty() || rounds[0].empty()) return;\n\n    const size_t nrounds = rounds.size();\n    const size_t ncores = rounds[0].size();\n\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_NUM_THREADS\") == nullptr && static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()) > ncores) {\n        omp_set_num_threads(static_cast<int>(ncores));\n    }\n\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_PROC_BIND\") != nullptr || getenv(\"OMP_PLACES\") != nullptr ||\n        getenv(\"GOMP_CPU_AFFINITY\") != nullptr || getenv(\"KMP_AFFINITY\") != nullptr) {\n        return; // user configured the placement, leave it alone\n    }\n\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const size_t n = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_num_threads());\n        const size_t tid = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_thread_num());\n\n        size_t r = 0;\n        size_t i = tid;\n        size_t perRound = n;\n        if (n > ncores) {\n            perRound = (n + nrounds - 1) / nrounds;\n            r = std::min(tid / perRound, nrounds - 1);\n            i = tid % perRound;\n        }\n        const std::vector<int>& ring = rounds[r];\n        const int cpu = ring[std::min(i * ring.size() / std::max<size_t>(perRound, 1), ring.size() - 1)];\n\n        cpu_set_t one;\n        CPU_ZERO(&one);\n        CPU_SET(cpu, &one);\n        pthread_setaffinity_np(pthread_self(), sizeof(one), &one);\n    }\n}\n\n// 3D index calculation\ninline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// True if v is an exact power of two, i.e. multiplying by 1/v gives bit-identical\n// results to dividing by v. Used to hoist the stencil divisions out of the loops\n// without changing the produced values.\nstatic bool isPowerOfTwo(const double v) noexcept {\n    if (!(v > 0.0) || std::isinf(v)) return false;\n    int e = 0;\n    return std::frexp(v, &e) == 0.5;\n}\n\n// Applies the 7-point Laplacian stencil (clamped boundaries) over the whole grid and\n// stores op(idx, center, laplacian) into dst. The (z, y) plane rows are distributed\n// over the OpenMP threads with a static schedule, which also matches the first-touch\n// page placement done at initialization time. This is a worksharing construct: it has\n// to be called from inside a parallel region.\ntemplate <bool Recip, typename Op>\nstatic void stencilSweep(const double* __restrict__ src, double* __restrict__ dst,\n                         const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                         const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, Op op) {\n    const double dx2 = dx * dx;\n    const double dy2 = dy * dy;\n    const double dz2 = dz * dz;\n    const double rdx2 = 1.0 / dx2;\n    const double rdy2 = 1.0 / dy2;\n    const double rdz2 = 1.0 / dz2;\n    const size_t nxy = nx * ny;\n\n    #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            const size_t zn = (z > 0) ? z - 1 : 0;\n            const size_t zp = (z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : z;\n            const size_t yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;\n            const size_t yp = (y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;\n\n            const size_t base = z * nxy + y * nx;\n            const double* __restrict__ c0 = src + base;\n            const double* __restrict__ cyn = src + z * nxy + yn * nx;\n            const double* __restrict__ cyp = src + z * nxy + yp * nx;\n            const double* __restrict__ czn = src + zn * nxy + y * nx;\n            const double* __restrict__ czp = src + zp * nxy + y * nx;\n            double* __restrict__ out = dst + base;\n\n            // Transverse (y/z) contribution is uniform along the row, the x direction\n            // only needs its neighbour clamping at the two ends of the row.\n            const auto body = [&](const size_t x, const size_t xn, const size_t xp) {\n                const double cv = c0[x];\n                const double sxx = c0[xp] + c0[xn] - 2.0 * cv;\n                const double syy = cyp[x] + cyn[x] - 2.0 * cv;\n                const double szz = czp[x] + czn[x] - 2.0 * cv;\n                const double cxx = Recip ? sxx * rdx2 : sxx / dx2;\n                const double cyy = Recip ? syy * rdy2 : syy / dy2;\n                const double czz = Recip ? szz * rdz2 : szz / dz2;\n                out[x] = op(base + x, cv, cxx + cyy + czz);\n            };\n\n            if (nx == 1) {\n                body(0, 0, 0);\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            body(0, 0, 1);\n            const size_t xend = nx - 1;\n            #pragma omp simd\n            for (size_t x = 1; x < xend; ++x) {\n                const double cv = c0[x];\n                const double sxx = c0[x + 1] + c0[x - 1] - 2.0 * cv;\n                const double syy = cyp[x] + cyn[x] - 2.0 * cv;\n                const double szz = czp[x] + czn[x] - 2.0 * cv;\n                const double cxx = Recip ? sxx * rdx2 : sxx / dx2;\n                const double cyy = Recip ? syy * rdy2 : syy / dy2;\n                const double czz = Recip ? szz * rdz2 : szz / dz2;\n                out[x] = op(base + x, cv, cxx + cyy + czz);\n            }\n            body(nx - 1, nx - 2, nx - 1);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Dispatch to the multiply-by-reciprocal variant whenever it is bit-exact\ntemplate <typename Op>\nstatic inline void stencilSweepDispatch(const double* __restrict__ src, double* __restrict__ dst,\n                                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                                        const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, Op op) {\n    if (isPowerOfTwo(dx * dx) && isPowerOfTwo(dy * dy) && isPowerOfTwo(dz * dz)) {\n        stencilSweep<true>(src, dst, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, op);\n    } else {\n        stencilSweep<false>(src, dst, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, op);\n    }\n}\n\n// Compute chemical potential\nvoid computeChemicalPotential(const double* __restrict__ c, double* __restrict__ mu,\n                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {\n    stencilSweepDispatch(c, mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz,\n                         [=](const size_t, const double cv, const double lap) {\n                             return 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)\n                                    + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * lap;\n                         });\n}\n\n// Cahn-Hilliard update step\nvoid cahnHilliardUpdate(double* __restrict__ cnew, const double* __restrict__ cold,\n                        const double* __restrict__ mu,\n                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {\n    stencilSweepDispatch(mu, cnew, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz,\n                         [=](const size_t idx, const double, const double lap) {\n                             return cold[idx] + dt * D * lap;\n                         });\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field (also performs the NUMA first touch of the buffer)\nvoid initializeConcentration(double* __restrict__ c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    #pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n                const size_t linear_id = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Touch a scratch buffer with the same static distribution used by the kernels\nstatic void firstTouch(double* __restrict__ p, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    #pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            double* __restrict__ row = p + z * nx * ny + y * nx;\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                row[x] = 0.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const double* __restrict__ c, const size_t gridSize) {\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    int bad = 0;\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(|:bad)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < gridSize; ++i) {\n        bad |= (std::isnan(c[i]) || std::isinf(c[i])) ? 1 : 0;\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min:minVal) reduction(max:maxVal)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < gridSize; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, c[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, c[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 64;\n    size_t ny = 0;\n    size_t nz = 0;\n    int iterations = 20;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i =
