If the user did not configure the OpenMP\n// affinity, pin the threads ourselves: one thread per physical core, spread over\n// all packages, using the SMT siblings only once every core is occupied. The stencil\n// already saturates the memory bandwidth with one thread per core, so the default\n// team size is capped at the number of cores unless the user asks for more.\nstatic void bindThreadsIfUnconfigured() {\n    cpu_set_t allowed;\n    CPU_ZERO(&allowed);\n    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(allowed), &allowed) != 0) return;\n\n    // Group the usable CPUs into \"rounds\": round r holds the r-th SMT sibling of\n    // every physical core, so round 0 already covers all cores exactly once.\n    std::vector<std::vector<int>> rounds;\n    std::vector<std::pair<long, long>> seenCores; // (package, core) -> round index by count\n    std::vector<int> coreCount;\n    for (int cpu = 0; cpu < CPU_SETSIZE; ++cpu) {\n        if (!CPU_ISSET(cpu, &allowed)) continue;\n\n        long pkg = 0;\n        long core = cpu;\n        char path[128];\n        snprintf(path, sizeof(path), \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/physical_package_id\", cpu);\n        if (FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\")) {\n            if (fscanf(f, \"%ld\", &pkg) != 1) pkg = 0;\n            fclose(f);\n        }\n        snprintf(path, sizeof(path), \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/core_id\", cpu);\n        if (FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\")) {\n            if (fscanf(f, \"%ld\", &core) != 1) core = cpu;\n            fclose(f);\n        }\n\n        size_t g = 0;\n        for (; g < seenCores.size(); ++g) {\n            if (seenCores[g].first == pkg && seenCores[g].second == core) break;\n        }\n        if (g == seenCores.size()) {\n            seenCores.emplace_back(pkg, core);\n            coreCount.push_back(0);\n        }\n        const size_t r = static_cast<size_t>(coreCount[g]++);\n        if (r >= rounds.size()) rounds.resize(r + 1);\n        rounds[r].push_back(cpu);\n    }\n    if (rounds.empty() || rounds[0].empty()) return;\n\n    const size_t nrounds = rounds.size();\n    const size_t ncores = rounds[0].size();\n\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_NUM_THREADS\") == nullptr && static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()) > ncores) {\n        omp_set_num_threads(static_cast<int>(ncores));\n    }\n\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_PROC_BIND\") != nullptr || getenv(\"OMP_PLACES\") != nullptr ||\n        getenv(\"GOMP_CPU_AFFINITY\") != nullptr || getenv(\"KMP_AFFINITY\") != nullptr) {\n        return; // user configured the placement, leave it alone\n    }\n\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const size_t n = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_num_threads());\n        const size_t tid = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_thread_num());\n\n        size_t r = 0;\n        size_t i = tid;\n        size_t perRound = n;\n        if (n > ncores) {\n            perRound = (n + nrounds - 1) / nrounds;\n            r = std::min(tid / perRound, nrounds - 1);\n            i = tid % perRound;\n        }\n        const std::vector<int>& ring = rounds[r];\n        const int cpu = ring[std::min(i * ring.size() / std::max<size_t>(perRound, 1), ring.size() - 1)];\n\n        cpu_set_t one;\n        CPU_ZERO(&one);\n        CPU_SET(cpu, &one);\n        pthread_setaffinity_np(pthread_self(), sizeof(one), &one);\n    }\n}\n\n// 3D index calculation\ninline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// True if v is an exact power of two, i.e. multiplying by 1/v gives bit-identical\n// results to dividing by v. Used to hoist the stencil divisions out of the loops\n// without changing the produced values.\nstatic bool isPowerOfTwo(const double v) noexcept {\n    if (!(v > 0.0) || std::isinf(v)) return false;\n    int e = 0;\n    return std::frexp(v, &e) == 0.5;\n}\n\n// Applies the 7-point Laplacian stencil (clamped boundaries) over the whole grid and\n// stores op(idx, center, laplacian) into dst. The (z, y) plane rows are distributed\n// over the OpenMP threads with a static schedule, which also matches the first-touch\n// page placement done at initialization time. This is a worksharing construct: it has\n// to be called from inside a parallel region.\ntemplate <bool Recip, typename Op>\nstatic void stencilSweep(const double* __restrict__ src, double* __restrict__ dst,\n                         const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                         const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, Op op) {\n    const double dx2 = dx * dx;\n    const double dy2 = dy * dy;\n    const double dz2 = dz * dz;\n    const double rdx2 = 1.0 / dx2;\n    const double rdy2 = 1.0 / dy2;\n    const double rdz2 = 1.0 / dz2;\n    const size_t nxy = nx * ny;\n\n    #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            const size_t zn = (z > 0) ? z - 1 : 0;\n            const size_t zp = (z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : z;\n            const size_t yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;\n            const size_t yp = (y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;\n\n            const size_t base = z * nxy + y * nx;\n            const double* __restrict__ c0 = src + base;\n            const double* __restrict__ cyn = src + z * nxy + yn * nx;\n            const double* __restrict__ cyp = src + z * nxy + yp * nx;\n            const double* __restrict__ czn = src + zn * nxy + y * nx;\n            const double* __restrict__ czp = src + zp * nxy + y * nx;\n            double* __restrict__ out = dst + base;\n\n            // Transverse (y/z) contribution is uniform along the row, the x direction\n            // only needs its neighbour clamping at the two ends of the row.\n            const auto body = [&](const size_t x, const size_t xn, const size_t xp) {\n                const double cv = c0[x];\n                const double sxx = c0[xp] + c0[xn] - 2.0 * cv;\n                const double syy = cyp[x] + cyn[x] - 2.0 * cv;\n                const double szz = czp[x] + czn[x] - 2.0 * cv;\n                const double cxx = Recip ? sxx * rdx2 : sxx / dx2;\n                const double cyy = Recip ? syy * rdy2 : syy / dy2;\n                const double czz = Recip ? szz * rdz2 : szz / dz2;\n                out[x] = op(base + x, cv, cxx + cyy + czz);\n            };\n\n            if (nx == 1) {\n                body(0, 0, 0);\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            body(0, 0, 1);\n            const size_t xend = nx - 1;\n            #pragma omp simd\n            for (size_t x = 1; x < xend; ++x) {\n                const double cv = c0[x];\n                const double sxx = c0[x + 1] + c0[x - 1] - 2.0 * cv;\n                const double syy = cyp[x] + cyn[x] - 2.0 * cv;\n                const double szz = czp[x] + czn[x] - 2.0 * cv;\n                const double cxx = Recip ? sxx * rdx2 : sxx / dx2;\n                const double cyy = Recip ? syy * rdy2 : syy / dy2;\n                const double czz = Recip ? szz * rdz2 : szz / dz2;\n                out[x] = op(base + x, cv, cxx + cyy + czz);\n            }\n            body(nx - 1, nx - 2, nx - 1);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Dispatch to the multiply-by-reciprocal variant whenever it is bit-exact\ntemplate <typename Op>\nstatic inline void stencilSweepDispatch(const double* __restrict__ src, double* __restrict__ dst,\n                                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                                        const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, Op op) {\n    if (isPowerOfTwo(dx * dx) && isPowerOfTwo(dy * dy) && isPowerOfTwo(dz * dz)) {\n        stencilSweep<true>(src, dst, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, op);\n    } else {\n        stencilSweep<false>(src, dst, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, op);\n    }\n}\n\n// Compute chemical potential\nvoid computeChemicalPotential(const double* __restrict__ c, double* __restrict__ mu,\n                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {\n    stencilSweepDispatch(c, mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz,\n                         [=](const size_t, const double cv, const double lap) {\n                             return 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)\n                                    + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * lap;\n                         });\n}\n\n// Cahn-Hilliard update step\nvoid cahnHilliardUpdate(double* __restrict__ cnew, const double* __restrict__ cold,\n                        const double* __restrict__ mu,\n                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {\n    stencilSweepDispatch(mu, cnew, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz,\n                         [=](const size_t idx, const double, const double lap) {\n                             return cold[idx] + dt * D * lap;\n                         });\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field (also performs the NUMA first touch of the buffer)\nvoid initializeConcentration(double* __restrict__ c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    #pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n                const size_t linear_id = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Touch a scratch buffer with the same static distribution used by the kernels\nstatic void firstTouch(double* __restrict__ p, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    #pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            double* __restrict__ row = p + z * nx * ny + y * nx;\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                row[x] = 0.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const double* __restrict__ c, const size_t gridSize) {\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    int bad = 0;\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(|:bad)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < gridSize; ++i) {\n        bad |= (std::isnan(c[i]) || std::isinf(c[i])) ? 1 : 0;\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min:minVal) reduction(max:maxVal)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < gridSize; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, c[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, c[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 64;\n    size_t ny = 0;\n    size_t nz = 0;\n    int iterations = 20;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-x\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-y\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-z\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n If the user did not configure the OpenMP\n// affinity, pin the threads ourselves: one thread per physical core, spread over\n// all packages, using the SMT siblings only once every core is occupied. The stencil\n// already saturates the memory bandwidth with one thread per core, so the default\n// team size is capped at the number of cores unless the user asks for more.\nstatic void bindThreadsIfUnconfigured() {\n    cpu_set_t allowed;\n    CPU_ZERO(&allowed);\n    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(allowed), &allowed) != 0) return;\n\n    // Group the usable CPUs into \"rounds\": round r holds the r-th SMT sibling of\n    // every physical core, so round 0 already covers all cores exactly once.\n    std::vector<std::vector<int>> rounds;\n    std::vector<std::pair<long, long>> seenCores; // (package, core) key per physical core\n    std::vector<int> siblingCount;\n    for (int cpu = 0; cpu < CPU_SETSIZE; ++cpu) {\n        if (!CPU_ISSET(cpu, &allowed)) continue;\n\n        long pkg = 0;\n        long core = cpu;\n        char path[128];\n        snprintf(path, sizeof(path), \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/physical_package_id\", cpu);\n        if (FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\")) {\n            if (fscanf(f, \"%ld\", &pkg) != 1) pkg = 0;\n            fclose(f);\n        }\n        snprintf(path, sizeof(path), \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/core_id\", cpu);\n        if (FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\")) {\n            if (fscanf(f, \"%ld\", &core) != 1) core = cpu;\n            fclose(f);\n        }\n\n        size_t g = 0;\n        for (; g < seenCores.size(); ++g) {\n            if (seenCores[g].first == pkg && seenCores[g].second == core) break;\n        }\n        if (g == seenCores.size()) {\n            seenCores.emplace_back(pkg, core);\n            siblingCount.push_back(0);\n        }\n        const size_t r = static_cast<size_t>(siblingCount[g]++);\n        if (r >= rounds.size()) rounds.resize(r + 1);\n        rounds[r].push_back(cpu);\n    }\n    if (rounds.empty() || rounds[0].empty()) return;\n\n    const size_t nrounds = rounds.size();\n    const size_t ncores = rounds[0].size();\n\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_NUM_THREADS\") == nullptr && static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()) > ncores) {\n        omp_set_num_threads(static_cast<int>(ncores));\n    }\n\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_PROC_BIND\") != nullptr || getenv(\"OMP_PLACES\") != nullptr ||\n        getenv(\"GOMP_CPU_AFFINITY\") != nullptr || getenv(\"KMP_AFFINITY\") != nullptr) {\n        return; // the user configured the placement, leave it alone\n    }\n\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const size_t n = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_num_threads());\n        const size_t tid = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_thread_num());\n\n        size_t r = 0;\n        size_t i = tid;\n        size_t perRound = n;\n        if (n > ncores) {\n            perRound = (n + nrounds - 1) / nrounds;\n            r = std::min(tid / perRound, nrounds - 1);\n            i = tid % perRound;\n        }\n        const std::vector<int>& ring = rounds[r];\n        const int cpu = ring[std::min(i * ring.size() / std::max<size_t>(perRound, 1), ring.size() - 1)];\n\n        cpu_set_t one;\n        CPU_ZERO(&one);\n        CPU_SET(cpu, &one);\n        pthread_setaffinity_np(pthread_self(), sizeof(one), &one);\n    }\n}\n\n// 3D index calculation\ninline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// True if v is an exact power of two, i.e. multiplying by 1/v gives bit-identical\n// results to dividing by v. Used to turn the stencil divisions into multiplications\n// without changing the produced values.\nstatic bool isPowerOfTwo(const double v) noexcept {\n    if (!(v > 0.0) || std::isinf(v)) return false;\n    int e = 0;\n    return std::frexp(v, &e) == 0.5;\n}\n\n// Constant simulation parameters, shared by all threads\nstruct Params {\n    size_t nx, ny, nz, nxy;\n    double dx2, dy2, dz2;\n    double rdx2, rdy2, rdz2;\n    double gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB;\n    double dtD;\n};\n\n// Applies the 7-point Laplacian to one row of nx cells and writes op(x, center, lap)\n// to out. The neighbour rows in y and z are passed in already clamped; only the x\n// direction needs its boundary handling, which is peeled off the vectorized body.\ntemplate <bool Recip, typename Op>\nstatic inline void rowSweep(const Params& p,\n                            const double* __restrict__ c0, const double* __restrict__ cyn,\n                            const double* __restrict__ cyp, const double* __restrict__ czn,\n                            const double* __restrict__ czp, double* __restrict__ out, Op op) {\n    const size_t nx = p.nx;\n\n    const auto edge = [&](const size_t x, const size_t xn, const size_t xp) {\n        const double cv = c0[x];\n        const double sxx = c0[xp] + c0[xn] - 2.0 * cv;\n        const double syy = cyp[x] + cyn[x] - 2.0 * cv;\n        const double szz = czp[x] + czn[x] - 2.0 * cv;\n        const double cxx = Recip ? sxx * p.rdx2 : sxx / p.dx2;\n        const double cyy = Recip ? syy * p.rdy2 : syy / p.dy2;\n        const double czz = Recip ? szz * p.rdz2 : szz / p.dz2;\n        out[x] = op(x, cv, cxx + cyy + czz);\n    };\n\n    if (nx == 1) {\n        edge(0, 0, 0);\n        return;\n    }\n\n    edge(0, 0, 1);\n    const size_t xend = nx - 1;\n    #pragma omp simd\n    for (size_t x = 1; x < xend; ++x) {\n        const double cv = c0[x];\n        const double sxx = c0[x + 1] + c0[x - 1] - 2.0 * cv;\n        const double syy = cyp[x] + cyn[x] - 2.0 * cv;\n        const double szz = czp[x] + czn[x] - 2.0 * cv;\n        const double cxx = Recip ? sxx * p.rdx2 : sxx / p.dx2;\n        const double cyy = Recip ? syy * p.rdy2 : syy / p.dy2;\n        const double czz = Recip ? szz * p.rdz2 : szz / p.dz2;\n        out[x] = op(x, cv, cxx + cyy + czz);\n    }\n    edge(nx - 1, nx - 2, nx - 1);\n}\n\n// A block of the grid owned by one thread: rows [y0, y1) of planes [z0, z1).\nstruct Tile {\n    size_t y0, y1, z0, z1;\n};\n\n// Fused Cahn-Hilliard time step over one tile.\n//\n// Instead of materializing the chemical potential for the whole grid (which costs a\n// full write plus a full read of a grid sized array per time step), mu is produced\n// plane by plane into a small rolling buffer of three planes that stays in cache: the\n// update of plane z only needs mu on planes z-1, z and z+1. Per cell this leaves one\n// streaming read of cold and one streaming write of cnew.\n//\n// mubuf holds the tile rows [ylo, yhi) of three consecutive planes, plane z living in\n// slot z % 3. The mu rows are computed one row wider than the tile on each side in y,\n// because the update needs mu at y-1 and y+1.\ntemplate <bool Recip>\nstatic void fusedTileStep(const Params& p, const Tile& tile,\n                          const double* __restrict__ cold, double* __restrict__ cnew,\n                          double* __restrict__ mubuf) {\n    const size_t nx = p.nx, ny = p.ny, nz = p.nz, nxy = p.nxy;\n    const size_t ylo = (tile.y0 > 0) ? tile.y0 - 1 : 0;\n    const size_t yhi = (tile.y1 < ny) ? tile.y1 + 1 : ny;\n    const size_t planeStride = nx * (yhi - ylo);\n\n    const double gamma = p.gamma, e_AA = p.e_AA, e_BB = p.e_BB, e_AB = p.e_AB;\n    const double dtD = p.dtD;\n\n    // mu for one plane of the (widened) tile rows\n    const auto muPlane = [&](const size_t z) {\n        const size_t zn = (z > 0) ? z - 1 : 0;\n        const size_t zp = (z + 1 < nz) ? z + 1 : z;\n        double* __restrict__ dst = mubuf + (z % 3) * planeStride;\n        for (size_t y = ylo; y < yhi; ++y) {\n            const size_t yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;\n            const size_t yp = (y + 1 < ny) ? y + 1 : y;\n            rowSweep<Recip>(p, cold + z * nxy + y * nx, cold + z * nxy + yn * nx,\n                            cold + z * nxy + yp * nx, cold + zn * nxy + y * nx,\n                            cold + zp * nxy + y * nx, dst + (y - ylo) * nx,\n                            [=](const size_t, const double cv, const double lap) {\n                                return 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)\n                                       + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * lap;\n                            });\n        }\n    };\n\n    // concentration update for one plane of the tile rows, from the buffered mu\n    const auto updatePlane = [&](const size_t z) {\n        const size_t zn = (z > 0) ? z - 1 : 0;\n        const size_t zp = (z + 1 < nz) ? z + 1 : z;\n        const double* __restrict__ m0 = mubuf + (z % 3) * planeStride;\n        const double* __restrict__ mzn = mubuf + (zn % 3) * planeStride;\n        const double* __restrict__ mzp = mubuf + (zp % 3) * planeStride;\n        for (size_t y = tile.y0; y < tile.y1; ++y) {\n            const size_t yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;\n            const size_t yp = (y + 1 < ny) ? y + 1 : y;\n            const double* __restrict__ coldRow = cold + z * nxy + y * nx;\n            rowSweep<Recip>(p, m0 + (y - ylo) * nx, m0 + (yn - ylo) * nx, m0 + (yp - ylo) * nx,\n                            mzn + (y - ylo) * nx, mzp + (y - ylo) * nx, cnew + z * nxy + y * nx,\n                            [=](const size_t x, const double, const double lap) {\n                                return coldRow[x] + dtD * lap;\n                            });\n        }\n    };\n\n    // Walk the planes of the tile, keeping the mu buffer one plane ahead.\n    size_t next = (tile.z0 > 0) ? tile.z0 - 1 : 0;\n    for (size_t z = tile.z0; z < tile.z1; ++z) {\n        const size_t need = (z + 1 < nz) ? z + 1 : nz - 1;\n        for (; next <= need; ++next) {\n            muPlane(next);\n        }\n        updatePlane(z);\n    }\n}\n\n// Splits the grid into one tile per thread. Threads are handed contiguous blocks of\n// y rows; if there are more threads than y blocks, the threads sharing a y block\n// split its planes among themselves. The y block size is chosen so that the three\n// rolling mu planes of a tile stay cache resident.\nstatic std::vector<Tile> buildTiles(const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const size_t nthreads) {\n    // three planes of nx * yb doubles should fit comfortably into the private caches\n    constexpr size_t planeBudget = 32768; // elements per rolling buffer\n    size_t yb = std::min(ny, std::max<size_t>(1, planeBudget / std::max<size_t>(nx, 1)));\n    size_t nyb = (ny + yb - 1) / yb;\n\n    // shrink the blocks if that is what it takes to keep every thread busy\n    const size_t neededBlocks = (nthreads + nz - 1) / nz;\n    while (nyb < neededBlocks && yb > 1) {\n        yb = (yb + 1) / 2;\n        nyb = (ny + yb - 1) / yb;\n    }\n\n    std::vector<Tile> tiles(nthreads);\n    for (size_t t = 0; t < nthreads; ++t) {\n        if (nthreads <= nyb) {\n            // one thread covers a contiguous range of y blocks over all planes\n            const size_t b0 = t * nyb / nthreads;\n            const size_t b1 = (t + 1) * nyb / nthreads;\n            tiles[t] = Tile{std::min(b0 * yb, ny), std::min(b1 * yb, ny), 0, nz};\n        } else {\n            // several threads share a y block and split its planes\n            const size_t b = t * nyb / nthreads;\n            const size_t first = b * nthreads / nyb;                  // first thread of the group\n            const size_t last = (b + 1) * nthreads / nyb;             // one past the last\n            const size_t group = std::max<size_t>(last - first, 1);\n            const size_t k = t - first;\n            tiles[t] = Tile{std::min(b * yb, ny), std::min((b + 1) * yb, ny),\n                            k * nz / group, (k + 1) * nz / group};\n        }\n    }\n    return tiles;\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field (this is also the NUMA first touch of the buffer)\nvoid initializeConcentration(double* __restrict__ c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                             const std::vector<Tile>& tiles) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const Tile& tile = tiles[omp_get_thread_num()];\n        for (size_t z = tile.z0; z < tile.z1; ++z) {\n            for (size_t y = tile.y0; y < tile.y1; ++y) {\n                for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                    // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n                    const size_t linear_id = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n                    const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                    c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Touch a buffer with the tile distribution used by the kernel, so that its pages end\n// up on the NUMA node of the thread that will work on them.\nstatic void firstTouch(double* __restrict__ p, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const std::vector<Tile>& tiles) {\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const Tile& tile = tiles[omp_get_thread_num()];\n        for (size_t z = tile.z0; z < tile.z1; ++z) {\n            for (size_t y = tile.y0; y < tile.y1; ++y) {\n                double* __restrict__ row = p + z * nx * ny + y * nx;\n                for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                    row[x] = 0.0;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const double* __restrict__ c, const size_t gridSize) {\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    int bad = 0;\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(|:bad)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < gridSize; ++i) {\n        bad |= (std::isnan(c[i]) || std::isinf(c[i])) ? 1 : 0;\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min:minVal) reduction(max:maxVal)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < gridSize; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, c[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, c[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 64;\n    size_t ny = 0;\n    size_t nz = 0;\n    int iterations = 20;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-x\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-y\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-z\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;\n    if steps: %d\\n\", iterations);\n    printf(\"Threads: %d\\n\", omp_get_max_threads());\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n\n    // Physical parameters\n    const e_AB = (2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double gamma = 0.5;\n    const double D = 1.0;\n\n    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;\n    if (gridSize == 0) {\n        printf(\"Empty grid\\n\");\n        return 1;\n    }\n\n    Params params;\n    params.nx = nx;\n    params.ny = ny;\n    params.nz = nz;\n    params.nxy = nx * ny;\n    params.dx2 = dx * dx;\n    params.dy2 = dy * dy;\n    params.dz2 = dz * dz;\n    params.rdx2 = 1.0 / params.dx2;\n    params.rdy2 = 1.0 / params.dy2;\n    params.rdz2 = 1.0 / params.dz2;\n    params.gamma = gamma;\n    params.e_AA = e_AA;\n    params.e_BB = e_BB;\n    params.e_AB = e_AB;\n    params.dtD = dt * D;\n    const bool recip = isPowerOfTwo(params.dx2) && isPowerOfTwo(params.dy2) && isPowerOfTwo(params.dz2);\n\n    const size_t nthreads = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads());\n    const std::vector<Tile> tiles = buildTiles(nx, ny, nz, nthreads);\n\n    // Allocate arrays. Raw allocations are used so that the pages are first touched by\n    // the thread that later works on them (NUMA locality).\n    const size_t bytes = ((gridSize * sizeof(double)) + 63) & ~size_t(63);\n    double* cold = static_cast<double*>(std::aligned_alloc(64, bytes));\n    double* cnew = static_cast<double*>(std::aligned_alloc(64, bytes));\n    if (cold == nullptr || cnew == nullptr) {\n        printf(\"Allocation failed\\n\");\n        return 1;\n    }\n    firstTouch(cnew, nx, ny, tiles);\n\n    // Initialize concentration field\n    printf(\"Initializing concentration field...\\n\");\n    initializeConcentration(cold, nx, ny, nz, tiles);\n\n    // Run simulation\n    printf(\"Running Cahn-Hilliard simulation...\\n\");\n    std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::time_point start, end;\n\n    // A single parallel region covers the whole time loop, so the team is created\n    // once and the per thread mu buffers are allocated (and first touched) once.\n    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(nthreads)\n    {\n        const Tile& tile = tiles[omp_get_thread_num()];\n        const size_t ylo = (tile.y0 > 0) ? tile.y0 - If the user did not configure the OpenMP\n// affinity, pin the threads ourselves: one thread per physical core, spread over\n// all packages, using the SMT siblings only once every core is occupied. The stencil\n// already saturates the memory bandwidth with one thread per core, so the default\n// team size is capped at the number of cores unless the user asks for more.\nstatic void bindThreadsIfUnconfigured() {\n    cpu_set_t allowed;\n    CPU_ZERO(&allowed);\n    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(allowed), &allowed) != 0) return;\n\n    // Group the usable CPUs into \"rounds\": round r holds the r-th SMT sibling of\n    // every physical core, so round 0 already covers all cores exactly once.\n    std::vector<std::vector<int>> rounds;\n    std::vector<std::pair<long, long>> seenCores; // (package, core) key per physical core\n    std::vector<int> siblingCount;\n    for (int cpu = 0; cpu < CPU_SETSIZE; ++cpu) {\n        if (!CPU_ISSET(cpu, &allowed)) continue;\n\n        long pkg = 0;\n        long core = cpu;\n        char path[128];\n        snprintf(path, sizeof(path), \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/physical_package_id\", cpu);\n        if (FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\")) {\n            if (fscanf(f, \"%ld\", &pkg) != 1) pkg = 0;\n            fclose(f);\n        }\n        snprintf(path, sizeof(path), \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/core_id\", cpu);\n        if (FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\")) {\n            if (fscanf(f, \"%ld\", &core) != 1) core = cpu;\n            fclose(f);\n        }\n\n        size_t g = 0;\n        for (; g < seenCores.size(); ++g) {\n            if (seenCores[g].first == pkg && seenCores[g].second == core) break;\n        }\n        if (g == seenCores.size()) {\n            seenCores.emplace_back(pkg, core);\n            siblingCount.push_back(0);\n        }\n        const size_t r = static_cast<size_t>(siblingCount[g]++);\n        if (r >= rounds.size()) rounds.resize(r + 1);\n        rounds[r].push_back(cpu);\n    }\n    if (rounds.empty() || rounds[0].empty()) return;\n\n    const size_t nrounds = rounds.size();\n    const size_t ncores = rounds[0].size();\n\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_NUM_THREADS\") == nullptr && static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()) > ncores) {\n        omp_set_num_threads(static_cast<int>(ncores));\n    }\n\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_PROC_BIND\") != nullptr || getenv(\"OMP_PLACES\") != nullptr ||\n        getenv(\"GOMP_CPU_AFFINITY\") != nullptr || getenv(\"KMP_AFFINITY\") != nullptr) {\n        return; // the user configured the placement, leave it alone\n    }\n\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const size_t n = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_num_threads());\n        const size_t tid = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_thread_num());\n\n        size_t r = 0;\n        size_t i = tid;\n        size_t perRound = n;\n        if (n > ncores) {\n            perRound = (n + nrounds - 1) / nrounds;\n            r = std::min(tid / perRound, nrounds - 1);\n            i = tid % perRound;\n        }\n        const std::vector<int>& ring = rounds[r];\n        const int cpu = ring[std::min(i * ring.size() / std::max<size_t>(perRound, 1), ring.size() - 1)];\n\n        cpu_set_t one;\n        CPU_ZERO(&one);\n        CPU_SET(cpu, &one);\n        pthread_setaffinity_np(pthread_self(), sizeof(one), &one);\n    }\n}\n\n// 3D index calculation\ninline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// True if v is an exact power of two, i.e. multiplying by 1/v gives bit-identical\n// results to dividing by v. Used to turn the stencil divisions into multiplications\n// without changing the produced values.\nstatic bool isPowerOfTwo(const double v) noexcept {\n    if (!(v > 0.0) || std::isinf(v)) return false;\n    int e = 0;\n    return std::frexp(v, &e) == 0.5;\n}\n\n// Constant simulation parameters, shared by all threads\nstruct Params {\n    size_t nx, ny, nz, nxy;\n    double dx2, dy2, dz2;\n    double rdx2, rdy2, rdz2;\n    double gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB;\n    double dtD;\n};\n\n// Applies the 7-point Laplacian to one row of nx cells and writes op(x, center, lap)\n// to out. The neighbour rows in y and z are passed in already clamped; only the x\n// direction needs its boundary handling, which is peeled off the vectorized body.\ntemplate <bool Recip, typename Op>\nstatic inline void rowSweep(const Params& p,\n                            const double* __restrict__ c0, const double* __restrict__ cyn,\n                            const double* __restrict__ cyp, const double* __restrict__ czn,\n                            const double* __restrict__ czp, double* __restrict__ out, Op op) {\n    const size_t nx = p.nx;\n\n    const auto edge = [&](const size_t x, const size_t xn, const size_t xp) {\n        const double cv = c0[x];\n        const double sxx = c0[xp] + c0[xn] - 2.0 * cv;\n        const double syy = cyp[x] + cyn[x] - 2.0 * cv;\n        const double szz = czp[x] + czn[x] - 2.0 * cv;\n        const double cxx = Recip ? sxx * p.rdx2 : sxx / p.dx2;\n        const double cyy = Recip ? syy * p.rdy2 : syy / p.dy2;\n        const double czz = Recip ? szz * p.rdz2 : szz / p.dz2;\n        out[x] = op(x, cv, cxx + cyy + czz);\n    };\n\n    if (nx == 1) {\n        edge(0, 0, 0);\n        return;\n    }\n\n    edge(0, 0, 1);\n    const size_t xend = nx - 1;\n    #pragma omp simd\n    for (size_t x = 1; x < xend; ++x) {\n        const double cv = c0[x];\n        const double sxx = c0[x + 1] + c0[x - 1] - 2.0 * cv;\n        const double syy = cyp[x] + cyn[x] - 2.0 * cv;\n        const double szz = czp[x] + czn[x] - 2.0 * cv;\n        const double cxx = Recip ? sxx * p.rdx2 : sxx / p.dx2;\n        const double cyy = Recip ? syy * p.rdy2 : syy / p.dy2;\n        const double czz = Recip ? szz * p.rdz2 : szz / p.dz2;\n        out[x] = op(x, cv, cxx + cyy + czz);\n    }\n    edge(nx - 1, nx - 2, nx - 1);\n}\n\n// A block of the grid owned by one thread: rows [y0, y1) of planes [z0, z1).\nstruct Tile {\n    size_t y0, y1, z0, z1;\n};\n\n// Fused Cahn-Hilliard time step over one tile.\n//\n// Instead of materializing the chemical potential for the whole grid (which costs a\n// full write plus a full read of a grid sized array per time step), mu is produced\n// plane by plane into a small rolling buffer of three planes that stays in cache: the\n// update of plane z only needs mu on planes z-1, z and z+1. Per cell this leaves one\n// streaming read of cold and one streaming write of cnew.\n//\n// mubuf holds the tile rows [ylo, yhi) of three consecutive planes, plane z living in\n// slot z % 3. The mu rows are computed one row wider than the tile on each side in y,\n// because the update needs mu at y-1 and y+1.\ntemplate <bool Recip>\nstatic void fusedTileStep(const Params& p, const Tile& tile,\n                          const double* __restrict__ cold, double* __restrict__ cnew,\n                          double* __restrict__ mubuf) {\n    const size_t nx = p.nx, ny = p.ny, nz = p.nz, nxy = p.nxy;\n    const size_t ylo = (tile.y0 > 0) ? tile.y0 - 1 : 0;\n    const size_t yhi = (tile.y1 < ny) ? tile.y1 + 1 : ny;\n    const size_t planeStride = nx * (yhi - ylo);\n\n    const double gamma = p.gamma, e_AA = p.e_AA, e_BB = p.e_BB, e_AB = p.e_AB;\n    const double dtD = p.dtD;\n\n    // mu for one plane of the (widened) tile rows\n    const auto muPlane = [&](const size_t z) {\n        const size_t zn = (z > 0) ? z - 1 : 0;\n        const size_t zp = (z + 1 < nz) ? z + 1 : z;\n        double* __restrict__ dst = mubuf + (z % 3) * planeStride;\n        for (size_t y = ylo; y < yhi; ++y) {\n            const size_t yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;\n            const size_t yp = (y + 1 < ny) ? y + 1 : y;\n            rowSweep<Recip>(p, cold + z * nxy + y * nx, cold + z * nxy + yn * nx,\n                            cold + z * nxy + yp * nx, cold + zn * nxy + y * nx,\n                            cold + zp * nxy + y * nx, dst + (y - ylo) * nx,\n                            [=](const size_t, const double cv, const double lap) {\n                                return 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)\n                                       + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * lap;\n                            });\n        }\n    };\n\n    // concentration update for one plane of the tile rows, from the buffered mu\n    const auto updatePlane = [&](const size_t z) {\n        const size_t zn = (z > 0) ? z - 1 : 0;\n        const size_t zp = (z + 1 < nz) ? z + 1 : z;\n        const double* __restrict__ m0 = mubuf + (z % 3) * planeStride;\n        const double* __restrict__ mzn = mubuf + (zn % 3) * planeStride;\n        const double* __restrict__ mzp = mubuf + (zp % 3) * planeStride;\n        for (size_t y = tile.y0; y < tile.y1; ++y) {\n            const size_t yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;\n            const size_t yp = (y + 1 < ny) ? y + 1 : y;\n            const double* __restrict__ coldRow = cold + z * nxy + y * nx;\n            rowSweep<Recip>(p, m0 + (y - ylo) * nx, m0 + (yn - ylo) * nx, m0 + (yp - ylo) * nx,\n                            mzn + (y - ylo) * nx, mzp + (y - ylo) * nx, cnew + z * nxy + y * nx,\n                            [=](const size_t x, const double, const double lap) {\n                                return coldRow[x] + dtD * lap;\n                            });\n        }\n    };\n\n    // Walk the planes of the tile, keeping the mu buffer one plane ahead.\n    size_t next = (tile.z0 > 0) ? tile.z0 - 1 : 0;\n    for (size_t z = tile.z0; z < tile.z1; ++z) {\n        const size_t need = (z + 1 < nz) ? z + 1 : nz - 1;\n        for (; next <= need; ++next) {\n            muPlane(next);\n        }\n        updatePlane(z);\n    }\n}\n\n// Splits the grid into one tile per thread. Threads are handed contiguous blocks of\n// y rows; if there are more threads than y blocks, the threads sharing a y block\n// split its planes among themselves. The y block size is chosen so that the three\n// rolling mu planes of a tile stay cache resident.\nstatic std::vector<Tile> buildTiles(const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const size_t nthreads) {\n    // three planes of nx * yb doubles should fit comfortably into the private caches\n    constexpr size_t planeBudget = 32768; // elements per rolling buffer\n    size_t yb = std::min(ny, std::max<size_t>(1, planeBudget / std::max<size_t>(nx, 1)));\n    size_t nyb = (ny + yb - 1) / yb;\n\n    // shrink the blocks if that is what it takes to keep every thread busy\n    const size_t neededBlocks = (nthreads + nz - 1) / nz;\n    while (nyb < neededBlocks && yb > 1) {\n        yb = (yb + 1) / 2;\n        nyb = (ny + yb - 1) / yb;\n    }\n\n    std::vector<Tile> tiles(nthreads);\n    for (size_t t = 0; t < nthreads; ++t) {\n        if (nthreads <= nyb) {\n            // one thread covers a contiguous range of y blocks over all planes\n            const size_t b0 = t * nyb / nthreads;\n            const size_t b1 = (t + 1) * nyb / nthreads;\n            tiles[t] = Tile{std::min(b0 * yb, ny), std::min(b1 * yb, ny), 0, nz};\n        } else {\n            // several threads share a y block and split its planes\n            const size_t b = t * nyb / nthreads;\n            const size_t first = b * nthreads / nyb;                  // first thread of the group\n            const size_t last = (b + 1) * nthreads / nyb;             // one past the last\n            const size_t group = std::max<size_t>(last - first, 1);\n            const size_t k = t - first;\n            tiles[t] = Tile{std::min(b * yb, ny), std::min((b + 1) * yb, ny),\n                            k * nz / group, (k + 1) * nz / group};\n        }\n    }\n    return tiles;\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field (this is also the NUMA first touch of the buffer)\nvoid initializeConcentration(double* __restrict__ c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                             const std::vector<Tile>& tiles) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const Tile& tile = tiles[omp_get_thread_num()];\n        for (size_t z = tile.z0; z < tile.z1; ++z) {\n            for (size_t y = tile.y0; y < tile.y1; ++y) {\n                for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                    // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n                    const size_t linear_id = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n                    const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                    c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Touch a buffer with the tile distribution used by the kernel, so that its pages end\n// up on the NUMA node of the thread that will work on them.\nstatic void firstTouch(double* __restrict__ p, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const std::vector<Tile>& tiles) {\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const Tile& tile = tiles[omp_get_thread_num()];\n        for (size_t z = tile.z0; z < tile.z1; ++z) {\n            for (size_t y = tile.y0; y < tile.y1; ++y) {\n                double* __restrict__ row = p + z * nx * ny + y * nx;\n                for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                    row[x] = 0.0;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const double* __restrict__ c, const size_t gridSize) {\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    int bad = 0;\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(|:bad)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < gridSize; ++i) {\n        bad |= (std::isnan(c[i]) || std::isinf(c[i])) ? 1 : 0;\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min:minVal) reduction(max:maxVal)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < gridSize; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, c[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, c[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 64;\n    size_t ny = 0;\n    size_t nz = 0;\n    int iterations = 20;\n    bool validate If the user did not configure the OpenMP\n// affinity, pin the threads ourselves: one thread per physical core, spread over\n// all packages, using the SMT siblings only once every core is occupied. The stencil\n// already saturates the memory bandwidth with one thread per core, so the default\n// team size is capped at the number of cores unless the user asks for more.\nstatic void bindThreadsIfUnconfigured() {\n    cpu_set_t allowed;\n    CPU_ZERO(&allowed);\n    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(allowed), &allowed) != 0) return;\n\n    // Group the usable CPUs into \"rounds\": round r holds the r-th SMT sibling of\n    // every physical core, so round 0 already covers all cores exactly once.\n    std::vector<std::vector<int>> rounds;\n    std::vector<std::pair<long, long>> seenCores; // (package, core) -> round index by count\n    std::vector<int> coreCount;\n    for (int cpu = 0; cpu < CPU_SETSIZE; ++cpu) {\n        if (!CPU_ISSET(cpu, &allowed)) continue;\n\n        long pkg = 0;\n        long core = cpu;\n        char path[128];\n        snprintf(path, sizeof(path), \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/physical_package_id\", cpu);\n        if (FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\")) {\n            if (fscanf(f, \"%ld\", &pkg) != 1) pkg = 0;\n            fclose(f);\n        }\n        snprintf(path, sizeof(path), \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/core_id\", cpu);\n        if (FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\")) {\n            if (fscanf(f, \"%ld\", &core) != 1) core = cpu;\n            fclose(f);\n        }\n\n        size_t g = 0;\n        for (; g < seenCores.size(); ++g) {\n            if (seenCores[g].first == pkg && seenCores[g].second == core) break;\n        }\n        if (g == seenCores.size()) {\n            seenCores.emplace_back(pkg, core);\n            coreCount.push_back(0);\n        }\n        const size_t r = static_cast<size_t>(coreCount[g]++);\n        if (r >= rounds.size()) rounds.resize(r + 1);\n        rounds[r].push_back(cpu);\n    }\n    if (rounds.empty() || rounds[0].empty()) return;\n\n    const size_t nrounds = rounds.size();\n    const size_t ncores = rounds[0].size();\n\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_NUM_THREADS\") == nullptr && static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()) > ncores) {\n        omp_set_num_threads(static_cast<int>(ncores));\n    }\n\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_PROC_BIND\") != nullptr || getenv(\"OMP_PLACES\") != nullptr ||\n        getenv(\"GOMP_CPU_AFFINITY\") != nullptr || getenv(\"KMP_AFFINITY\") != nullptr) {\n        return; // user configured the placement, leave it alone\n    }\n\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const size_t n = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_num_threads());\n        const size_t tid = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_thread_num());\n\n        size_t r = 0;\n        size_t i = tid;\n        size_t perRound = n;\n        if (n > ncores) {\n            perRound = (n + nrounds - 1) / nrounds;\n            r = std::min(tid / perRound, nrounds - 1);\n            i = tid % perRound;\n        }\n        const std::vector<int>& ring = rounds[r];\n        const int cpu = ring[std::min(i * ring.size() / std::max<size_t>(perRound, 1), ring.size() - 1)];\n\n        cpu_set_t one;\n        CPU_ZERO(&one);\n        CPU_SET(cpu, &one);\n        pthread_setaffinity_np(pthread_self(), sizeof(one), &one);\n    }\n}\n\n// 3D index calculation\ninline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// True if v is an exact power of two, i.e. multiplying by 1/v gives bit-identical\n// results to dividing by v. Used to hoist the stencil divisions out of the loops\n// without changing the produced values.\nstatic bool isPowerOfTwo(const double v) noexcept {\n    if (!(v > 0.0) || std::isinf(v)) return false;\n    int e = 0;\n    return std::frexp(v, &e) == 0.5;\n}\n\n// Applies the 7-point Laplacian stencil (clamped boundaries) over the whole grid and\n// stores op(idx, center, laplacian) into dst. The (z, y) plane rows are distributed\n// over the OpenMP threads with a static schedule, which also matches the first-touch\n// page placement done at initialization time. This is a worksharing construct: it has\n// to be called from inside a parallel region.\ntemplate <bool Recip, typename Op>\nstatic void stencilSweep(const double* __restrict__ src, double* __restrict__ dst,\n                         const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                         const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, Op op) {\n    const double dx2 = dx * dx;\n    const double dy2 = dy * dy;\n    const double dz2 = dz * dz;\n    const double rdx2 = 1.0 / dx2;\n    const double rdy2 = 1.0 / dy2;\n    const double rdz2 = 1.0 / dz2;\n    const size_t nxy = nx * ny;\n\n    #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            const size_t zn = (z > 0) ? z - 1 : 0;\n            const size_t zp = (z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : z;\n            const size_t yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;\n            const size_t yp = (y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;\n\n            const size_t base = z * nxy + y * nx;\n            const double* __restrict__ c0 = src + base;\n            const double* __restrict__ cyn = src + z * nxy + yn * nx;\n            const double* __restrict__ cyp = src + z * nxy + yp * nx;\n            const double* __restrict__ czn = src + zn * nxy + y * nx;\n            const double* __restrict__ czp = src + zp * nxy + y * nx;\n            double* __restrict__ out = dst + base;\n\n            // Transverse (y/z) contribution is uniform along the row, the x direction\n            // only needs its neighbour clamping at the two ends of the row.\n            const auto body = [&](const size_t x, const size_t xn, const size_t xp) {\n                const double cv = c0[x];\n                const double sxx = c0[xp] + c0[xn] - 2.0 * cv;\n                const double syy = cyp[x] + cyn[x] - 2.0 * cv;\n                const double szz = czp[x] + czn[x] - 2.0 * cv;\n                const double cxx = Recip ? sxx * rdx2 : sxx / dx2;\n                const double cyy = Recip ? syy * rdy2 : syy / dy2;\n                const double czz = Recip ? szz * rdz2 : szz / dz2;\n                out[x] = op(base + x, cv, cxx + cyy + czz);\n            };\n\n            if (nx == 1) {\n                body(0, 0, 0);\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            body(0, 0, 1);\n            const size_t xend = nx - 1;\n            #pragma omp simd\n            for (size_t x = 1; x < xend; ++x) {\n                const double cv = c0[x];\n                const double sxx = c0[x + 1] + c0[x - 1] - 2.0 * cv;\n                const double syy = cyp[x] + cyn[x] - 2.0 * cv;\n                const double szz = czp[x] + czn[x] - 2.0 * cv;\n                const double cxx = Recip ? sxx * rdx2 : sxx / dx2;\n                const double cyy = Recip ? syy * rdy2 : syy / dy2;\n                const double czz = Recip ? szz * rdz2 : szz / dz2;\n                out[x] = op(base + x, cv, cxx + cyy + czz);\n            }\n            body(nx - 1, nx - 2, nx - 1);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Dispatch to the multiply-by-reciprocal variant whenever it is bit-exact\ntemplate <typename Op>\nstatic inline void stencilSweepDispatch(const double* __restrict__ src, double* __restrict__ dst,\n                                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                                        const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, Op op) {\n    if (isPowerOfTwo(dx * dx) && isPowerOfTwo(dy * dy) && isPowerOfTwo(dz * dz)) {\n        stencilSweep<true>(src, dst, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, op);\n    } else {\n        stencilSweep<false>(src, dst, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, op);\n    }\n}\n\n// Compute chemical potential\nvoid computeChemicalPotential(const double* __restrict__ c, double* __restrict__ mu,\n                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {\n    stencilSweepDispatch(c, mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz,\n                         [=](const size_t, const double cv, const double lap) {\n                             return 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)\n                                    + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * lap;\n                         });\n}\n\n// Cahn-Hilliard update step\nvoid cahnHilliardUpdate(double* __restrict__ cnew, const double* __restrict__ cold,\n                        const double* __restrict__ mu,\n                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {\n    stencilSweepDispatch(mu, cnew, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz,\n                         [=](const size_t idx, const double, const double lap) {\n                             return cold[idx] + dt * D * lap;\n                         });\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field (also performs the NUMA first touch of the buffer)\nvoid initializeConcentration(double* __restrict__ c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    #pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n                const size_t linear_id = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Touch a scratch buffer with the same static distribution used by the kernels\nstatic void firstTouch(double* __restrict__ p, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    #pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            double* __restrict__ row = p + z * nx * ny + y * nx;\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                row[x] = 0.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const double* __restrict__ c, const size_t gridSize) {\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    int bad = 0;\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(|:bad)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < gridSize; ++i) {\n        bad |= (std::isnan(c[i]) || std::isinf(c[i])) ? 1 : 0;\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min:minVal) reduction(max:maxVal)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < gridSize; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, c[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, c[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n If the user did not configure the OpenMP\n// affinity, pin the threads ourselves: one thread per physical core, spread over\n// all packages, using the SMT siblings only once every core is occupied. The stencil\n// already saturates the memory bandwidth with one thread per core, so the default\n// team size is capped at the number of cores unless the user asks for more.\nstatic void bindThreadsIfUnconfigured() {\n    cpu_set_t allowed;\n    CPU_ZERO(&allowed);\n    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(allowed), &allowed) != 0) return;\n\n    // Group the usable CPUs into \"rounds\": round r holds the r-th SMT sibling of\n    // every physical core, so round 0 already covers all cores exactly once.\n    std::vector<std::vector<int>> rounds;\n    std::vector<std::pair<long, long>> seenCores; // (package, core) key per physical core\n    std::vector<int> siblingCount;\n    for (int cpu = 0; cpu < CPU_SETSIZE; ++cpu) {\n        if (!CPU_ISSET(cpu, &allowed)) continue;\n\n        long pkg = 0;\n        long core = cpu;\n        char path[128];\n        snprintf(path, sizeof(path), \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/physical_package_id\", cpu);\n        if (FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\")) {\n            if (fscanf(f, \"%ld\", &pkg) != 1) pkg = 0;\n            fclose(f);\n        }\n        snprintf(path, sizeof(path), \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/core_id\", cpu);\n        if (FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\")) {\n            if (fscanf(f, \"%ld\", &core) != 1) core = cpu;\n            fclose(f);\n        }\n\n        size_t g = 0;\n        for (; g < seenCores.size(); ++g) {\n            if (seenCores[g].first == pkg && seenCores[g].second == core) break;\n        }\n        if (g == seenCores.size()) {\n            seenCores.emplace_back(pkg, core);\n            siblingCount.push_back(0);\n        }\n        const size_t r = static_cast<size_t>(siblingCount[g]++);\n        if (r >= rounds.size()) rounds.resize(r + 1);\n        rounds[r].push_back(cpu);\n    }\n    if (rounds.empty() || rounds[0].empty()) return;\n\n    const size_t nrounds = rounds.size();\n    const size_t ncores = rounds[0].size();\n\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_NUM_THREADS\") == nullptr && static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()) > ncores) {\n        omp_set_num_threads(static_cast<int>(ncores));\n    }\n\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_PROC_BIND\") != nullptr || getenv(\"OMP_PLACES\") != nullptr ||\n        getenv(\"GOMP_CPU_AFFINITY\") != nullptr || getenv(\"KMP_AFFINITY\") != nullptr) {\n        return; // the user configured the placement, leave it alone\n    }\n\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const size_t n = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_num_threads());\n        const size_t tid = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_thread_num());\n\n        size_t r = 0;\n        size_t i = tid;\n        size_t perRound = n;\n        if (n > ncores) {\n            perRound = (n + nrounds - 1) / nrounds;\n            r = std::min(tid / perRound, nrounds - 1);\n            i = tid % perRound;\n        }\n        const std::vector<int>& ring = rounds[r];\n        const int cpu = ring[std::min(i * ring.size() / std::max<size_t>(perRound, 1), ring.size() - 1)];\n\n        cpu_set_t one;\n        CPU_ZERO(&one);\n        CPU_SET(cpu, &one);\n        pthread_setaffinity_np(pthread_self(), sizeof(one), &one);\n    }\n}\n\n// 3D index calculation\ninline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// True if v is an exact power of two, i.e. multiplying by 1/v gives bit-identical\n// results to dividing by v. Used to turn the stencil divisions into multiplications\n// without changing the produced values.\nstatic bool isPowerOfTwo(const double v) noexcept {\n    if (!(v > 0.0) || std::isinf(v)) return false;\n    int e = 0;\n    return std::frexp(v, &e) == 0.5;\n}\n\n// Constant simulation parameters, shared by all threads\nstruct Params {\n    size_t nx, ny, nz, nxy;\n    double dx2, dy2, dz2;\n    double rdx2, rdy2, rdz2;\n    double gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB;\n    double dtD;\n};\n\n// Applies the 7-point Laplacian to one row of nx cells and writes op(x, center, lap)\n// to out. The neighbour rows in y and z are passed in already clamped; only the x\n// direction needs its boundary handling, which is peeled off the vectorized body.\ntemplate <bool Recip, typename Op>\nstatic inline void rowSweep(const Params& p,\n                            const double* __restrict__ c0, const double* __restrict__ cyn,\n                            const double* __restrict__ cyp, const double* __restrict__ czn,\n                            const double* __restrict__ czp, double* __restrict__ out, Op op) {\n    const size_t nx = p.nx;\n\n    const auto edge = [&](const size_t x, const size_t xn, const size_t xp) {\n        const double cv = c0[x];\n        const double sxx = c0[xp] + c0[xn] - 2.0 * cv;\n        const double syy = cyp[x] + cyn[x] - 2.0 * cv;\n        const double szz = czp[x] + czn[x] - 2.0 * cv;\n        const double cxx = Recip ? sxx * p.rdx2 : sxx / p.dx2;\n        const double cyy = Recip ? syy * p.rdy2 : syy / p.dy2;\n        const double czz = Recip ? szz * p.rdz2 : szz / p.dz2;\n        out[x] = op(x, cv, cxx + cyy + czz);\n    };\n\n    if (nx == 1) {\n        edge(0, 0, 0);\n        return;\n    }\n\n    edge(0, 0, 1);\n    const size_t xend = nx - 1;\n    #pragma omp simd\n    for (size_t x = 1; x < xend; ++x) {\n        const double cv = c0[x];\n        const double sxx = c0[x + 1] + c0[x - 1] - 2.0 * cv;\n        const double syy = cyp[x] + cyn[x] - 2.0 * cv;\n        const double szz = czp[x] + czn[x] - 2.0 * cv;\n        const double cxx = Recip ? sxx * p.rdx2 : sxx / p.dx2;\n        const double cyy = Recip ? syy * p.rdy2 : syy / p.dy2;\n        const double czz = Recip ? szz * p.rdz2 : szz / p.dz2;\n        out[x] = op(x, cv, cxx + cyy + czz);\n    }\n    edge(nx - 1, nx - 2, nx - 1);\n}\n\n// A block of the grid owned by one thread: rows [y0, y1) of planes [z0, z1).\nstruct Tile {\n    size_t y0, y1, z0, z1;\n};\n\n// Fused Cahn-Hilliard time step over one tile.\n//\n// Instead of materializing the chemical potential for the whole grid (which costs a\n// full write plus a full read of a grid sized array per time step), mu is produced\n// plane by plane into a small rolling buffer of three planes that stays in cache: the\n// update of plane z only needs mu on planes z-1, z and z+1. Per cell this leaves one\n// streaming read of cold and one streaming write of cnew.\n//\n// mubuf holds the tile rows [ylo, yhi) of three consecutive planes, plane z living in\n// slot z % 3. The mu rows are computed one row wider than the tile on each side in y,\n// because the update needs mu at y-1 and y+1.\ntemplate <bool Recip>\nstatic void fusedTileStep(const Params& p, const Tile& tile,\n                          const double* __restrict__ cold, double* __restrict__ cnew,\n                          double* __restrict__ mubuf) {\n    const size_t nx = p.nx, ny = p.ny, nz = p.nz, nxy = p.nxy;\n    const size_t ylo = (tile.y0 > 0) ? tile.y0 - 1 : 0;\n    const size_t yhi = (tile.y1 < ny) ? tile.y1 + 1 : ny;\n    const size_t planeStride = nx * (yhi - ylo);\n\n    const double gamma = p.gamma, e_AA = p.e_AA, e_BB = p.e_BB, e_AB = p.e_AB;\n    const double dtD = p.dtD;\n\n    // mu for one plane of the (widened) tile rows\n    const auto muPlane = [&](const size_t z) {\n        const size_t zn = (z > 0) ? z - 1 : 0;\n        const size_t zp = (z + 1 < nz) ? z + 1 : z;\n        double* __restrict__ dst = mubuf + (z % 3) * planeStride;\n        for (size_t y = ylo; y < yhi; ++y) {\n            const size_t yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;\n            const size_t yp = (y + 1 < ny) ? y + 1 : y;\n            rowSweep<Recip>(p, cold + z * nxy + y * nx, cold + z * nxy + yn * nx,\n                            cold + z * nxy + yp * nx, cold + zn * nxy + y * nx,\n                            cold + zp * nxy + y * nx, dst + (y - ylo) * nx,\n                            [=](const size_t, const double cv, const double lap) {\n                                return 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)\n                                       + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * lap;\n                            });\n        }\n    };\n\n    // concentration update for one plane of the tile rows, from the buffered mu\n    const auto updatePlane = [&](const size_t z) {\n        const size_t zn = (z > 0) ? z - 1 : 0;\n        const size_t zp = (z + 1 < nz) ? z + 1 : z;\n        const double* __restrict__ m0 = mubuf + (z % 3) * planeStride;\n        const double* __restrict__ mzn = mubuf + (zn % 3) * planeStride;\n        const double* __restrict__ mzp = mubuf + (zp % 3) * planeStride;\n        for (size_t y = tile.y0; y < tile.y1; ++y) {\n            const size_t yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;\n            const size_t yp = (y + 1 < ny) ? y + 1 : y;\n            const double* __restrict__ coldRow = cold + z * nxy + y * nx;\n            rowSweep<Recip>(p, m0 + (y - ylo) * nx, m0 + (yn - ylo) * nx, m0 + (yp - ylo) * nx,\n                            mzn + (y - ylo) * nx, mzp + (y - ylo) * nx, cnew + z * nxy + y * nx,\n                            [=](const size_t x, const double, const double lap) {\n                                return coldRow[x] + dtD * lap;\n                            });\n        }\n    };\n\n    // Walk the planes of the tile, keeping the mu buffer one plane ahead.\n    size_t next = (tile.z0 > 0) ? tile.z0 - 1 : 0;\n    for (size_t z = tile.z0; z < tile.z1; ++z) {\n        const size_t need = (z + 1 < nz) ? z + 1 : nz - 1;\n        for (; next <= need; ++next) {\n            muPlane(next);\n        }\n        updatePlane(z);\n    }\n}\n\n// Splits the grid into one tile per thread. Threads are handed contiguous blocks of\n// y rows; if there are more threads than y blocks, the threads sharing a y block\n// split its planes among themselves. The y block size is chosen so that the three\n// rolling mu planes of a tile stay cache resident.\nstatic std::vector<Tile> buildTiles(const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const size_t nthreads) {\n    // three planes of nx * yb doubles should fit comfortably into the private caches\n    constexpr size_t planeBudget = 32768; // elements per rolling buffer\n    size_t yb = std::min(ny, std::max<size_t>(1, planeBudget / std::max<size_t>(nx, 1)));\n    size_t nyb = (ny + yb - 1) / yb;\n\n    // shrink the blocks if that is what it takes to keep every thread busy\n    const size_t neededBlocks = (nthreads + nz - 1) / nz;\n    while (nyb < neededBlocks && yb > 1) {\n        yb = (yb + 1) / 2;\n        nyb = (ny + yb - 1) / yb;\n    }\n\n    std::vector<Tile> tiles(nthreads);\n    for (size_t t = 0; t < nthreads; ++t) {\n        if (nthreads <= nyb) {\n            // one thread covers a contiguous range of y blocks over all planes\n            const size_t b0 = t * nyb / nthreads;\n            const size_t b1 = (t + 1) * nyb / nthreads;\n            tiles[t] = Tile{std::min(b0 * yb, ny), std::min(b1 * yb, ny), 0, nz};\n        } else {\n            // several threads share a y block and split its planes\n            const size_t b = t * nyb / nthreads;\n            const size_t first = b * nthreads / nyb;                  // first thread of the group\n            const size_t last = (b + 1) * nthreads / nyb;             // one past the last\n            const size_t group = std::max<size_t>(last - first, 1);\n            const size_t k = t - first;\n            tiles[t] = Tile{std::min(b * yb, ny), std::min((b + 1) * yb, ny),\n                            k * nz / group, (k + 1) * nz / group};\n        }\n    }\n    return tiles;\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field (this is also the NUMA first touch of the buffer)\nvoid initializeConcentration(double* __restrict__ c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                             const std::vector<Tile>& tiles) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const Tile& tile = tiles[omp_get_thread_num()];\n        for (size_t z = tile.z0; z < tile.z1; ++z) {\n            for (size_t y = tile.y0; y < tile.y1; ++y) {\n                for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                    // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n                    const size_t linear_id = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n                    const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                    c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Touch a buffer with the tile distribution used by the kernel, so that its pages end\n// up on the NUMA node of the thread that will work on them.\nstatic void firstTouch(double* __restrict__ p, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const std::vector<Tile>& tiles) {\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const Tile& tile = tiles[omp_get_thread_num()];\n        for (size_t z = tile.z0; z < tile.z1; ++z) {\n            for (size_t y = tile.y0; y < tile.y1; ++y) {\n                double* __restrict__ row = p + z * nx * ny + y * nx;\n                for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                    row[x] = 0.0;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const double* __restrict__ c, const size_t gridSize) {\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    int bad = 0;\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(|:bad)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < gridSize; ++i) {\n        bad |= (std::isnan(c[i]) || std::isinf(c[i])) ? 1 : 0;\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min:minVal) reduction(max:maxVal)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < gridSize; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, c[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, c[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 64;\n    size_t ny = 0;\n    size_t nz = 0;\n    int iterations = 20;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-x\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-y\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-z\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;\n    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;\n\n    bindThreadsIfUnconfigured();\n\n    printf(\"Cahn-Hilliard Phase Separation Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\\n\", nx, ny, nz);\n    printf(\"Time steps: %d\\n\", iterations);\n    printf(\"Threads: %d\\n\", omp_get_max_threads());\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n\n    // Physical parameters\n    const double dx = 1.0;\n    const double dy = 1.0;\n    const double dz = 1.0;\n    const double dt = 0.01;\n    const double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double e_AB = (2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double gamma = 0.5;\n    const double D = 1.0;\n\n    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;\n    if (gridSize == 0) {\n        printf(\"Empty grid\\n\");\n        return 1;\n    }\n\n    Params params;\n    params.nx = nx;\n    params.ny = ny;\n    params.nz = nz;\n    params.nxy = nx * ny;\n    params.dx2 = dx * dx;\n    params.dy2 = dy * dy;\n    params.dz2 = dz * dz;\n    params.rdx2 = 1.0 / params.dx2;\n    params.rdy2 = 1.0 / params.dy2;\n    params.rdz2 = 1.0 / params.dz2;\n    params.gamma = gamma;\n    params.e_AA = e_AA;\n    params.e_BB = e_BB;\n    params.e_AB = e_AB;\n    params.dtD = dt * D;\n    const bool recip = isPowerOfTwo(params.dx2) && isPowerOfTwo(params.dy2) && isPowerOfTwo(params.dz2);\n\n    const size_t nthreads = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads());\n    const std::vector<Tile> tiles = buildTiles(nx, ny, nz, nthreads);\n\n    // Allocate arrays. Raw allocations are used so that the pages are first touched by\n    // the thread that later works on them (NUMA locality).\n    const size_t bytes = ((gridSize * sizeof(double)) + 63) & ~size_t(63);\n    double* cold = static_cast<double*>(std::aligned_alloc(64, bytes));\n    double* cnew = static_cast<double*>(std::aligned_alloc(64, bytes));\n    if (cold == nullptr || cnew == nullptr) {\n        printf(\"Allocation failed\\n\");\n        return 1;\n    }\n    firstTouch(cnew, nx, ny, tiles);\n\n    // Initialize concentration field\n    printf(\"Initializing concentration field...\\n\");\n    initializeConcentration(cold, nx, ny, nz, tiles);\n\n    // Run simulation\n    printf(\"Running Cahn-Hilliard simulation...\\n\");\n    std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::time_point start, end;\n\n    // A single parallel region covers the whole time loop, so the team is created\n    // once and the per thread mu buffers are allocated (and first touched) once.\n    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(nthreads)\n    {\n        const Tile& tile = tiles[omp_get_thread_num()];\n        const size_t ylo = (tile.y0 > 0) ? tile.y0 - 1 : 0;\n        const size_t yhi = (tile.y1 < ny) ? tile.y1 + 1 : ny;\n        std::vector<double> mubuf(3 * nx * (yhi > ylo ? yhi - ylo : 0), 0.0);\n        double* buf = mubuf.data();\n\n        #pragma omp barrier\n        #pragma omp master\n        start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n        #pragma omp barrier\n\n        for (int t = 0; t < iterations; ++t) {\n            if (tile.z0 < tile.z1 && tile.y0 < tile.y1) {\n                if (recip) {\n                    fusedTileStep<true>(params, tile, cold, cnew, buf);\n                } else {\n                    fusedTileStep<false>(params, tile, cold, cnew, buf);\n                }\n            }\n\n            // Swap buffers once every thread is done with the current step\n            #pragma omp barrier\n            #pragma omp single\n            std::swap(cold, cnew);\n        }\n\n        #pragma omp barrier\n        #pragma omp master\n        end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    }\n\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n    // Calculate performance\n    double cellUpdates = (double)gridSize * iterations;\n    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\\n\", mcups);\n\n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        print_results(std::vector<double>(cold, cold + gridSize), \"Concentration\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    int rc = 0;\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        bool valid = validateResult(cold, gridSize);\n\n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            rc = 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    std::free(cold);\n    std::free(cnew);\n    return rc;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":253,"oldLines":23,"newStart":253,"newLines":26,"lines":["         nyb = (ny + yb - 1) / yb;","     }"," ","-    std::vector<Tile> tiles(nthreads);","-    for (size_t t = 0;
